# M2b 导入 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** CSV / Excel 导入到已有表或新建表（列映射、类型校验、追加 / 清空后导入 / 按主键更新、整体事务或分批提交、出错停止或跳过并生成出错报告），执行 SQL 文件（后台任务，边读边拆分，扫描后确认危险语句），以及对象树的"导出数据"入口。

**Architecture:** 文件在后端解析（浏览器模式先上传，桌面版用本机路径）：`dblens-dialect` 生成各方言的批量写入语句和建表语句，并提供分块拆分 SQL 的 `StreamSplitter`；`dblens-service` 读 CSV / Excel、推断类型、转换值，导入和执行 SQL 文件都是后台任务（另开连接，`TaskManager` 管理状态、取消、确认和报告）；前端是三步的导入向导、执行 SQL 文件对话框和右下角的任务通知。

**Tech Stack:** Rust（axum、tokio、csv、calamine、encoding_rs、encoding_rs_io）；React 19 + TypeScript、antd 6、zustand、@tanstack/react-query 5、Vitest。

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-27-m2b-import-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- 文件在后端解析；浏览器模式先上传到数据目录的 `imports/`，桌面版直接读本机路径；本机路径只有桌面程序的本机服务接受。
- 导入和执行 SQL 文件都另开连接，开始前经过执行策略（拒绝时不开始）；生产环境的连接开始前由用户确认。
- 整体一个事务时任何错误全部回滚（新建的表一并删除）；清空后导入固定为整体一个事务；分批提交时出错可停止或跳过，跳过的行写入出错报告。
- 依赖只用正式发布版，跟随最新；新版发布满 7 天再升级。
- 前端给用户看的文字都用词条（中英文），英文区分单复数；后端错误文字用 `msg!` 词条；日志不翻译。
- 代码注释用中文；文件 UTF-8、LF 换行；前端代码风格：无分号、单引号、两格缩进。
- cargo 命令加 `--config "source.mirror.registry='sparse+https://rsproxy.cn/index/'"`（clippy 写在 `clippy` 之后），并设 `CARGO_INCREMENTAL=0`；后端测试在 `DBLENS_LANG=en` 下运行。
- 前端工具用 PowerShell 执行（`npm`/`npx` 在本机不可用，用 `pnpm exec`）。
- 测试数据库的密码只在命令行中以内联环境变量传入，不写进仓库、报告或提交信息。

## 与设计文档的差异（写计划时实测后调整）

- Oracle、达梦的按主键更新每行一条 MERGE：多行用 `UNION ALL` 拼来源时，各行 NULL 参数的类型不一致会报错。
- 导出仍用 M1c3 的 `ExportManager`，导入和 SQL 文件用新的 `TaskManager`：避免改动已实测的导出。
- 预览接口为 `POST /imports/preview`（不需要连接）；预览单元格的校验在前端用 M2a 的解析规则。
- 上传上限的设置项为 `ui.import.upload_limit_mb`：接口只允许写 `ui.` 开头的设置。
- 对象树导出整张表时不排序（与打开数据标签相同）。
- 另加依赖 `encoding_rs_io`（转码的读取器）。

## 执行说明

任务 2–10 的代码都已在临时副本中逐任务实现并通过检查（含 5 个实库；浏览器实测覆盖了导入到新表和已有表、按主键更新加跳过、出错报告、执行 SQL 文件、生产环境确认、导出数据入口），执行时按顺序重放。新文件给出全文，修改的文件给出 diff（基于上一个任务完成后的状态，可直接 `git apply`）。任务 4 改了 `Cargo.lock`（新依赖，由 cargo 自动更新，计划中不列出）；`web/pnpm-lock.yaml` 不变。

## 文件结构

```text
crates/dblens-dialect/src/{split.rs, import.rs, ddl.rs, lib.rs}      分块拆分；导入语句与建表语句（新增 import.rs）
crates/dblens-core/locales/app.json                                  后端词条
crates/dblens-service/src/import/{mod.rs, file.rs, values.rs, job.rs}  文件读取、预览、推断、转换、导入任务（新增）
crates/dblens-service/src/{task.rs, sqlfile.rs}                       任务管理与上传、执行 SQL 文件（新增）
crates/dblens-service/src/{service.rs, session.rs, fake.rs, lib.rs, export/mod.rs}; tests/live.rs
crates/dblens-api/src/{tasks.rs, exports.rs, lib.rs}; tests/api.rs     接口（新增 tasks.rs）
crates/dblens-desktop/src/{shell.rs, main.rs}                        打开对话框
web/src/shared/{api.ts, shell.ts}                                    接口客户端、打开对话框
web/src/features/import/                                             导入向导、SQL 文件对话框、任务通知（新增）
web/src/{app/{menus.ts, TitleBar.tsx, Shell.tsx, ui.ts, shell.css}, features/{tree/ObjectTree.tsx, data/DataView.tsx}}   入口
web/src/locales/{zh-CN,en}/{import.json, app.json, tree.json, editor.json, common.json}, locales/index.ts   词条
docs/notes/{m2b-import.md, m0-4b-api.md}; docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-22-dblens-design.md
```

**Files:** 视复查结果而定（`web/package.json`、`Cargo.toml` 及锁文件）。

本机经代理上网，Node 的 `fetch` 默认不读 `HTTPS_PROXY`，必须先设 `NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY=1`，否则全部查询失败，最后仍显示"0 个可升级"。

```powershell
$env:PATH = "D:\Environments\nvm4w\nodejs;$env:PATH"; $env:NODE_USE_ENV_PROXY = '1'; node scripts/check-deps.mjs
```

Expected：输出中没有"查询失败"。2026-10-27 写计划时：共 77 个直接依赖，0 个可升级（9 个有新版但发布未满 7 天）。以执行当天的结果为准。

输出中 `->` 的都升级：npm 用 `pnpm add 包@版本`（在 `web` 目录），Cargo 改工作区 `Cargo.toml` 后执行 `cargo update -p 包`。然后跑全量检查：

### Task 2: 方言：分块拆分 SQL

`dblens-dialect/src/split.rs` 改为可以分块喂入的 `StreamSplitter`（执行 SQL 文件时边读边拆）：
- `feed(&str) -> Vec<SqlStatement>` 返回本块中已经完整的语句，`finish(self) -> Vec<SqlStatement>` 返回文本末尾剩下的；
- 块末尾不足以判断的结构（被切开的结束符、未闭合的字符串或注释、未读到换行的行首命令、PL/SQL 块开头的关键字）返回 `Step::Wait`，等下一块；能提前确定不是命令的行不必等到换行；
- 每块之后丢掉当前语句之前的文本（多留 4 个字节供向前查看），内存只与当前语句的长度有关；
- `SqlStatement` 增加 `line`（语句开头的行号，从 1 开始）；`split_statements` 改为一次喂入全文。

测试：已有用例的文本按随机长度切块、以及在每个字符位置切成两块喂入，结果与整段拆分完全一致；行号和位置；语句一完整就交出。

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dblens-dialect/src/lib.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-dialect/src/split.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces（`dblens_dialect`）：
  - `StreamSplitter::new(Family)`、`feed(&mut self, &str) -> Vec<SqlStatement>`、`finish(self) -> Vec<SqlStatement>`；
  - `SqlStatement { text, start, end, line: usize }`（`start`/`end` 为全部已喂入文本中的字节位置）。

```diff
+//! 以及 Oracle/达梦的 PL/SQL 块和 `/` 结束符。保留每条语句在原文中的字节范围和起始行号，便于定位出错位置。
+//!
+//! 可以分块喂入（`StreamSplitter`，执行 SQL 文件用）：跨块保留结束符、PL/SQL 块、字符串和注释的状态，
+//! 从上次停下的位置继续扫描。块末尾不足以判断的结构（被切开的结束符、未闭合的字符串或注释、
+//! 未读到换行的行首命令、PL/SQL 块开头的关键字）等下一块再判断，文本结束时按结束处理；
+//! 因此任意切块方式的结果都与整段拆分相同。`split_statements` 就是一次喂入全文。

 //! ## 已知限制
 //! - Oracle/达梦的 PL/SQL 块必须有单独一行的 `/` 才会结束；没有的话，后面的语句会被并入（与 SQL*Plus 一致）。

+//! - 分块喂入时，跨越多块的单个字符串、注释或 `$$` 函数体，每来一块都从它的开头重新查找结尾。

+
+    /// 按伪随机长度（1..=max 字节，切在字符边界上）把文本切块喂入。
+    fn split_in_chunks(sql: &str, family: Family, seed: u64, max: usize) -> Vec<SqlStatement> {
+        let mut s = StreamSplitter::new(family);
+        let mut out = Vec::new();
+        let mut rng = seed;
+        let mut pos = 0;
+        while pos < sql.len() {
+            rng = rng.wrapping_mul(6364136223846793005).wrapping_add(1442695040888963407);
+            let mut end = (pos + 1 + (rng >> 33) as usize % max).min(sql.len());
+            while !sql.is_char_boundary(end) {
+                end += 1;
+            }
+            out.extend(s.feed(&sql[pos..end]));
+            pos = end;
+        }
+        out.extend(s.finish());
+        out
+    }
+
+    const FAMILIES: [Family; 4] = [Family::MySql, Family::Postgres, Family::Oracle, Family::Dameng];
+
+    /// 上面各测试用过的文本，加上结构可能恰好被切开的例子。
+    const SAMPLES: &[&str] = &[
+        "SELECT 1; SELECT 2",
+        "  SELECT 1 ;\n\n",
+        ";;; \n ;",
+        "-- 只有注释\n/* 还有 */",
+        "SELECT '数洞;'; SELECT 2",
+        "SELECT 'a;b'; SELECT \"c;d\"; SELECT `e;f`",
+        "SELECT 'it''s; ok', 'a\\';b'; SELECT 2",
+        "-- x;\nSELECT 1; # y;\nSELECT 2 /* ; */;",
+        "SELECT 5--2; SELECT 1",
+        "DELIMITER $$\nCREATE PROCEDURE p() BEGIN SELECT 1; SELECT 2; END$$\nDELIMITER ;\nSELECT 3;",
+        "SELECT 0;\ndelimiter //\nSELECT 1//",
+        "SELECT a,\n  delimiter AS d FROM t",
+        "CREATE FUNCTION f() RETURNS int AS $$ BEGIN RETURN 1; END; $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql; SELECT 1",
+        "DO $body$ BEGIN PERFORM 1; END $body$; SELECT 2",
+        "SELECT $1; SELECT 2",
+        "SELECT E'a\\';b'; SELECT 2",
+        "SELECT 'a\\'; SELECT 2",
+        "/* a /* b */ ; */ SELECT 1; SELECT 2",
+        "SELECT 5--2;\nSELECT 1",
+        "BEGIN\n  NULL;\n  NULL;\nEND;\n/\nSELECT 1 FROM DUAL;",
+        "CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE p IS\nBEGIN\n  NULL;\nEND;\n/\n",
+        "create or replace editionable function f return number is begin return 1; end;\n/",
+        "SELECT 1 FROM DUAL\n/\nSELECT 2 FROM DUAL\n  /  \n",
+        "SELECT 4 / 2 FROM DUAL;",
+        "SELECT q'[a;b]' FROM DUAL; SELECT Q'#c;d#' FROM DUAL",
+        "SELECT nq'[it's;x]' FROM dual; SELECT 2 FROM dual",
+        "DECLARE\n  x INT;\nBEGIN\n  x := 1;\nEND;\n/\nSELECT 1;",
+        "SELECT 'abc; SELECT 2",
+        "\u{feff}SELECT 1;\u{3000}SELECT 2\u{3000}",
+        "DELIMITER\nSELECT 1;",
+        "-- 注释;\nSELECT '数据;\\' FROM t; /* 块 */ # 井\nDELIMITER //\n\
+         SELECT $$体;$$, $a$标;$a$, q'[中;]', nq'<x>' FROM DUAL//\nDELIMITER ;\n\
+         SELECT \"双;\", `反;` ;\nBEGIN NULL; END;\n/\n\u{3000}SELECT 1 -- 行尾\u{feff}",
+        "SELECT 1;\r\nSELECT 2;\r\n/\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE\r\n  PACKAGE pk AS x INT; END;\r\n/\r\n",
+        "INSERT INTO t VALUES ('a''b', E'\\\\', $q$x$q$, 'c'); -- 末尾注释",
+        "SELECT 1 /*+ hint */; SELECT /*! 2 */; #",
+    ];
+
+    #[test]
+    fn chunked_feeding_matches_whole_text() {
+        for sql in SAMPLES {
+            for family in FAMILIES {
+                let whole = split_statements(sql, family);
+                for max in [1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 64] {
+                    for seed in 0..10 {
+                        assert_eq!(
+                            split_in_chunks(sql, family, seed, max),
+                            whole,
+                            "{family:?} max={max} seed={seed}：{sql:?}"
+                        );
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn every_two_chunk_split_matches_whole_text() {
+        for sql in SAMPLES {
+            for family in FAMILIES {
+                let whole = split_statements(sql, family);
+                for (k, _) in sql.char_indices().skip(1) {
+                    let mut s = StreamSplitter::new(family);
+                    let mut out = s.feed(&sql[..k]);
+                    out.extend(s.feed(&sql[k..]));
+                    out.extend(s.finish());
+                    assert_eq!(out, whole, "{family:?} 切在 {k}：{sql:?}");
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn line_numbers_and_offsets_across_chunks() {
+        let sql = "SELECT 1;\nSELECT 2;\n\n  -- c\n  SELECT\n3;\nSELECT '多\n行'; SELECT 4";
+        let lines: Vec<usize> = split_statements(sql, Family::Postgres).iter().map(|s| s.line).collect();
+        assert_eq!(lines, [1, 2, 4, 7, 8]);
+        // 长文本分块喂入：位置和行号是全文中的
+        let big: String = (0..2000).map(|n| format!("INSERT INTO t VALUES ({n}, '行{n}');\n")).collect();
+        let out = split_in_chunks(&big, Family::MySql, 7, 4096);
+        assert_eq!(out.len(), 2000);
+        for (n, s) in out.iter().enumerate() {
+            assert_eq!(&big[s.start..s.end], s.text);
+            assert_eq!(s.line, n + 1);
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn statements_come_out_as_soon_as_complete() {
+        let mut s = StreamSplitter::new(Family::MySql);
+        assert!(s.feed("SELECT 1").is_empty());
+        let done = s.feed("; SELECT 2;\nSELECT");
+        assert_eq!(done.iter().map(|s| s.text.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["SELECT 1", "SELECT 2"]);
+        assert!(s.feed(" 3").is_empty());
+        assert_eq!(s.finish()[0].text, "SELECT 3");
+    }
 }
```

---

### Task 3: 方言：导入的写入语句与建表语句

`dblens-dialect/src/import.rs`：
- `WriteMode`（`append` / `replace` / `upsert`）；`ImportStatement { sql, params, rows }`；
- `Dialect::import_statements(schema, table, columns, rows, mode)`：每条至多 500 行、30000 个参数；
  - 追加：多行 `INSERT … VALUES (…), (…)`；Oracle 用 `INSERT ALL … SELECT 1 FROM DUAL`；
  - 按主键更新：MySQL 系 `ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE c = VALUES(c)`，PG 系 `ON CONFLICT (主键) DO UPDATE SET c = EXCLUDED.c`，Oracle/达梦每行一条 `MERGE`（多行用 UNION ALL 时各行 NULL 参数类型不一致会报错）；只映射了主键列时已存在的行不变；
  - PG：精确小数 `$n::text::numeric`（同 M2a），时间间隔和不认识的类型 `$n::text::原生类型`；
  - 列不存在、生成列、按主键更新时主键列没有全部映射、值的个数不对，返回 `invalid`；
- `delete_all_sql`（`DELETE FROM`，不用 TRUNCATE：MySQL、Oracle 中隐式提交）；`create_table_sql`（复用 `table_create`，去掉结尾分号）。

后端词条加 `import.no_columns`、`import.upsert_needs_key`、`import.row_width`。

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dblens-core/locales/app.json`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-dialect/src/ddl.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-dialect/src/import.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-dialect/src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces（`dblens_dialect`）：
  - `WriteMode::{Append, Replace, Upsert}`（serde snake_case）；
  - `ImportStatement { sql: String, params: Vec<Value>, rows: Range<usize> }`；
  - `Dialect::import_statements(&self, schema: &str, table: &Table, columns: &[String], rows: &[Vec<Value>], mode: WriteMode) -> Result<Vec<ImportStatement>>`；
  - `Dialect::delete_all_sql(&self, schema: &str, table: &str) -> String`、`Dialect::create_table_sql(&self, schema: &str, table: &Table) -> String`；
  - `MAX_ROWS_PER_STATEMENT = 500`、`MAX_PARAMS_PER_STATEMENT = 30000`。

```rust
//! 导入（M2b 设计文档 5.1 节）：由若干行生成写入语句（带占位符和参数），以及新建表的建表语句。
//! 一批行按每条语句至多 500 行、30000 个参数拆成多条。

use std::ops::Range;

use dblens_core::schema::{Column, Table};
use dblens_core::{Error, LogicalType, Result, Value, msg};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::{Dialect, Family};

/// 每条语句至多包含的行数。
pub const MAX_ROWS_PER_STATEMENT: usize = 500;
/// 每条语句至多包含的参数个数。
pub const MAX_PARAMS_PER_STATEMENT: usize = 30000;

/// 写入方式。清空后导入（`Replace`）的写入语句与追加相同，清空另用 `delete_all_sql`。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum WriteMode {
    Append,
    Replace,
    /// 按主键更新：存在则更新，不存在则插入。
    Upsert,
}

/// 一条写入语句。`rows` 是它包含的行在输入中的下标范围。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct ImportStatement {
    pub sql: String,
    pub params: Vec<Value>,
    pub rows: Range<usize>,
}

impl Dialect {
    /// 清空表：`DELETE FROM 表`。不用 TRUNCATE：它在 MySQL、Oracle 中隐式提交，不能回滚。
    pub fn delete_all_sql(&self, schema: &str, table: &str) -> String {
        format!("DELETE FROM {}", self.qualified(Some(schema), table))
    }

    /// 新建表的建表语句（列、可空、主键），不带结尾的分号。
    pub fn create_table_sql(&self, schema: &str, table: &Table) -> String {
        self.table_create(schema, table).trim_end().trim_end_matches(';').to_string()
    }

    /// 由若干行生成写入语句。`columns` 是映射到的表列，每行的值与之一一对应。
    /// 列不存在、是生成列，或按主键更新时表没有主键、主键列没有全部映射，返回 `invalid`。
    pub fn import_statements(
        &self,
        schema: &str,
        table: &Table,
        columns: &[String],
        rows: &[Vec<Value>],
        mode: WriteMode,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ImportStatement>> {
        if columns.is_empty() {
            return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.no_columns")));
        }
        let cols: Vec<&Column> = columns
            .iter()
            .map(|name| {
                let c = table
                    .columns
                    .iter()
                    .find(|c| &c.name == name)
                    .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid(msg!("changes.unknown_column", column = name)))?;
                if c.generated.is_some() {
                    return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("changes.generated_column", column = name)));
                }
                Ok(c)
            })
            .collect::<Result<_>>()?;
        let key: Vec<usize> = if mode == WriteMode::Upsert {
            let pk = table.primary_key.as_ref().filter(|pk| !pk.columns.is_empty());
            let pos: Option<Vec<usize>> =
                pk.and_then(|pk| pk.columns.iter().map(|k| columns.iter().position(|c| c == k)).collect());
            pos.ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid(msg!("import.upsert_needs_key", table = table.name)))?
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        };
        let merge = mode == WriteMode::Upsert && matches!(self.family(), Family::Oracle | Family::Dameng);
        // Oracle、达梦的 MERGE 每行一条：多行要用 UNION ALL 拼来源，各行的 NULL 参数类型不一致时会报错
        let per_statement =
            if merge { 1 } else { (MAX_PARAMS_PER_STATEMENT / cols.len()).clamp(1, MAX_ROWS_PER_STATEMENT) };
        let target = self.qualified(Some(schema), &table.name);
        let names: Vec<String> = cols.iter().map(|c| self.ident(&c.name)).collect();
        let list = names.join(", ");
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut start = 0;
        while start < rows.len() {
            let end = (start + per_statement).min(rows.len());
            let mut b = Binder { dialect: self, sql: String::new(), params: Vec::new() };
            if merge {
                b.merge(&target, &cols, &names, &key, &rows[start])?;
            } else {
                b.insert(&target, &cols, &list, &rows[start..end])?;
                if mode == WriteMode::Upsert {
                    b.upsert_tail(&names, &key);
                }
            }
            out.push(ImportStatement { sql: b.sql, params: b.params, rows: start..end });
            start = end;
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}

/// 拼语句，同时收集参数。
struct Binder<'a> {
    dialect: &'a Dialect,
    sql: String,
    params: Vec<Value>,
}

impl Binder<'_> {
    /// 一个参数。PG 驱动不能直接绑定 NUMERIC 参数，精确小数按文本传、由数据库转换（与表数据编辑相同）；
    /// 时间间隔和不认识的类型也按文本传，转换成列的原生类型。
    fn param(&mut self, col: &Column, v: &Value) {
        self.params.push(v.clone());
        self.sql.push_str(&self.dialect.placeholder(self.params.len()));
        if self.dialect.family() != Family::Postgres || matches!(v, Value::Null) {
            return;
        }
        if matches!(v, Value::Decimal(_)) {
            self.sql.push_str("::text::numeric");
        } else if matches!(col.data_type.logical, LogicalType::Interval | LogicalType::Other) {
            self.sql.push_str("::text::");
            self.sql.push_str(&col.data_type.native);
        }
    }

    fn row_values(&mut self, cols: &[&Column], row: &[Value]) -> Result<()> {
        if row.len() != cols.len() {
            return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.row_width", expected = cols.len(), actual = row.len())));
        }
        for (k, (c, v)) in cols.iter().zip(row).enumerate() {
            if k > 0 {
                self.sql.push_str(", ");
            }
            self.param(c, v);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 多行 INSERT。Oracle 用 `INSERT ALL`（多行 VALUES 要 23ai 才支持）。
    fn insert(&mut self, target: &str, cols: &[&Column], list: &str, rows: &[Vec<Value>]) -> Result<()> {
        if self.dialect.family() == Family::Oracle {
            self.sql.push_str("INSERT ALL");
            for row in rows {
                self.sql.push_str(&format!(" INTO {target} ({list}) VALUES ("));
                self.row_values(cols, row)?;
                self.sql.push(')');
            }
            self.sql.push_str(" SELECT 1 FROM DUAL");
            return Ok(());
        }
        self.sql.push_str(&format!("INSERT INTO {target} ({list}) VALUES "));
        for (n, row) in rows.iter().enumerate() {
            if n > 0 {
                self.sql.push_str(", ");
            }
            self.sql.push('(');
            self.row_values(cols, row)?;
            self.sql.push(')');
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// MySQL、PG 的按主键更新：接在多行 INSERT 后面。只映射了主键列时，已存在的行不变。
    fn upsert_tail(&mut self, names: &[String], key: &[usize]) {
        let others: Vec<&String> =
            names.iter().enumerate().filter(|(k, _)| !key.contains(k)).map(|(_, n)| n).collect();
        match self.dialect.family() {
            // 用 `VALUES(列)` 兼容 MariaDB；没有其他列时把主键列更新为自身，相当于不变
            Family::MySql => {
                let sets: Vec<String> = if others.is_empty() {
                    vec![format!("{0} = {0}", names[key[0]])]
                } else {
                    others.iter().map(|n| format!("{n} = VALUES({n})")).collect()
                };
                self.sql.push_str(&format!(" ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE {}", sets.join(", ")));
            }
            _ => {
                let keys: Vec<&str> = key.iter().map(|&k| names[k].as_str()).collect();
                self.sql.push_str(&format!(" ON CONFLICT ({}) DO ", keys.join(", ")));
                if others.is_empty() {
                    self.sql.push_str("NOTHING");
                } else {
                    let sets: Vec<String> = others.iter().map(|n| format!("{n} = EXCLUDED.{n}")).collect();
                    self.sql.push_str(&format!("UPDATE SET {}", sets.join(", ")));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Oracle、达梦的按主键更新：一行一条 MERGE。只映射了主键列时省略 WHEN MATCHED。
    fn merge(
        &mut self,
        target: &str,
        cols: &[&Column],
        names: &[String],
        key: &[usize],
        row: &[Value],
    ) -> Result<()> {
        if row.len() != cols.len() {
            return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.row_width", expected = cols.len(), actual = row.len())));
        }
        self.sql.push_str(&format!("MERGE INTO {target} t USING (SELECT "));
        for (k, (c, v)) in cols.iter().zip(row).enumerate() {
            if k > 0 {
                self.sql.push_str(", ");
            }
            self.param(c, v);
            self.sql.push_str(&format!(" AS {}", names[k]));
        }
        let on: Vec<String> = key.iter().map(|&k| format!("t.{0} = s.{0}", names[k])).collect();
        self.sql.push_str(&format!(" FROM DUAL) s ON ({})", on.join(" AND ")));
        let sets: Vec<String> = (0..names.len())
            .filter(|k| !key.contains(k))
            .map(|k| format!("t.{0} = s.{0}", names[k]))
            .collect();
        if !sets.is_empty() {
            self.sql.push_str(&format!(" WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET {}", sets.join(", ")));
        }
        let values: Vec<String> = names.iter().map(|n| format!("s.{n}")).collect();
        self.sql.push_str(&format!(
            " WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT ({}) VALUES ({})",
            names.join(", "),
            values.join(", ")
        ));
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use dblens_core::ColumnType;
    use dblens_core::schema::PrimaryKey;

    use super::*;
    use crate::Variant;

    fn col(name: &str, logical: LogicalType) -> Column {
        Column {
            name: name.into(),
            ordinal: 0,
            data_type: ColumnType { native: "x".into(), logical },
            nullable: true,
            default: None,
            comment: None,
            auto_increment: false,
            generated: None,
        }
    }

    /// orders(id 主键, name, price 精确小数, total 生成列)
    fn orders() -> Table {
        let mut total = col("total", LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some(10), scale: Some(2) });
        total.generated = Some("price * 2".into());
        Table {
            name: "orders".into(),
            columns: vec![
                col("id", LogicalType::Int { bits: 32 }),
                col("name", LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(20) }),
                col("price", LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some(10), scale: Some(2) }),
                total,
            ],
            primary_key: Some(PrimaryKey { name: None, columns: vec!["id".into()] }),
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }

    fn names(cols: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
        cols.iter().map(|c| c.to_string()).collect()
    }

    fn rows() -> Vec<Vec<Value>> {
        vec![
            vec![Value::Int(1), Value::String("a".into()), Value::Decimal("1.50".into())],
            vec![Value::Int(2), Value::Null, Value::Null],
        ]
    }

    fn sql(v: Variant, schema: &str, mode: WriteMode) -> Vec<String> {
        Dialect::new(v)
            .import_statements(schema, &orders(), &names(&["id", "name", "price"]), &rows(), mode)
            .unwrap()
            .into_iter()
            .map(|s| s.sql)
            .collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn append_uses_multi_row_insert() {
        assert_eq!(
            sql(Variant::Postgres, "public", WriteMode::Append),
            ["INSERT INTO public.orders (id, name, price) VALUES ($1, $2, $3::text::numeric), ($4, $5, $6)"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            sql(Variant::MySql, "shop", WriteMode::Replace),
            ["INSERT INTO shop.orders (id, name, price) VALUES (?, ?, ?), (?, ?, ?)"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            sql(Variant::Dameng, "SHOP", WriteMode::Append),
            [r#"INSERT INTO SHOP."orders" ("id", "name", "price") VALUES (?, ?, ?), (?, ?, ?)"#]
        );
        // Oracle：INSERT ALL
        assert_eq!(
            sql(Variant::Oracle, "HR", WriteMode::Append),
            [concat!(
                r#"INSERT ALL INTO HR."orders" ("id", "name", "price") VALUES (:1, :2, :3)"#,
                r#" INTO HR."orders" ("id", "name", "price") VALUES (:4, :5, :6) SELECT 1 FROM DUAL"#
            )]
        );
        let st = Dialect::new(Variant::Postgres)
            .import_statements(
                "public",
                &orders(),
                &names(&["id", "name", "price"]),
                &rows(),
                WriteMode::Append,
            )
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st[0].params, rows().concat());
        assert_eq!(st[0].rows, 0..2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn upsert_per_family() {
        assert_eq!(
            sql(Variant::MySql, "shop", WriteMode::Upsert),
            ["INSERT INTO shop.orders (id, name, price) VALUES (?, ?, ?), (?, ?, ?) \
              ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE name = VALUES(name), price = VALUES(price)"]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            sql(Variant::Postgres, "public", WriteMode::Upsert),
            ["INSERT INTO public.orders (id, name, price) VALUES ($1, $2, $3::text::numeric), ($4, $5, $6) \
              ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET name = EXCLUDED.name, price = EXCLUDED.price"]
        );
        // Oracle、达梦：一行一条 MERGE
        let ora = sql(Variant::Oracle, "HR", WriteMode::Upsert);
        assert_eq!(ora.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(
            ora[0],
            concat!(
                r#"MERGE INTO HR."orders" t USING (SELECT :1 AS "id", :2 AS "name", :3 AS "price" FROM DUAL) s"#,
                r#" ON (t."id" = s."id") WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET t."name" = s."name", t."price" = s."price""#,
                r#" WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT ("id", "name", "price") VALUES (s."id", s."name", s."price")"#
            )
        );
        let dm = sql(Variant::Dameng, "SHOP", WriteMode::Upsert);
        assert!(dm[1].starts_with(r#"MERGE INTO SHOP."orders" t USING (SELECT ? AS "id""#), "{}", dm[1]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn upsert_with_only_key_columns_keeps_existing_rows() {
        let only = |v: Variant| {
            Dialect::new(v)
                .import_statements("s", &orders(), &names(&["id"]), &[vec![Value::Int(1)]], WriteMode::Upsert)
                .unwrap()[0]
                .sql
                .clone()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            only(Variant::MySql),
            "INSERT INTO s.orders (id) VALUES (?) ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE id = id"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            only(Variant::Postgres),
            "INSERT INTO s.orders (id) VALUES ($1) ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            only(Variant::Oracle),
            concat!(
                r#"MERGE INTO "s"."orders" t USING (SELECT :1 AS "id" FROM DUAL) s ON (t."id" = s."id")"#,
                r#" WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT ("id") VALUES (s."id")"#
            )
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn splits_by_rows_and_params() {
        let d = Dialect::new(Variant::MySql);
        let many: Vec<Vec<Value>> =
            (0..1200).map(|n| vec![Value::Int(n), Value::Null, Value::Null]).collect();
        let cols = names(&["id", "name", "price"]);
        let st = d.import_statements("s", &orders(), &cols, &many, WriteMode::Append).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st.iter().map(|s| s.rows.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [0..500, 500..1000, 1000..1200]);
        assert_eq!(st[2].params.len(), 600);
        // 列多时按参数个数限制每条的行数：100 列 → 每条 300 行
        let wide = Table {
            columns: (0..100).map(|k| col(&format!("c{k}"), LogicalType::Int { bits: 32 })).collect(),
            ..orders()
        };
        let cols: Vec<String> = (0..100).map(|k| format!("c{k}")).collect();
        let rows: Vec<Vec<Value>> = (0..700).map(|_| vec![Value::Null; 100]).collect();
        let st = d.import_statements("s", &wide, &cols, &rows, WriteMode::Append).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st.iter().map(|s| s.rows.len()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [300, 300, 100]);
        assert!(st.iter().all(|s| s.params.len() <= MAX_PARAMS_PER_STATEMENT));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_bad_input() {
        let d = Dialect::new(Variant::Postgres);
        let t = orders();
        let err = |cols: &[&str], rows: &[Vec<Value>], mode| {
            d.import_statements("s", &t, &names(cols), rows, mode).unwrap_err()
        };
        let one = [vec![Value::Int(1)]];
        assert!(matches!(err(&[], &[], WriteMode::Append), Error::Invalid { .. }), "没有列");
        assert!(matches!(err(&["nope"], &one, WriteMode::Append), Error::Invalid { .. }), "不存在的列");
        assert!(matches!(err(&["total"], &one, WriteMode::Append), Error::Invalid { .. }), "生成列");
        assert!(matches!(err(&["name"], &one, WriteMode::Upsert), Error::Invalid { .. }), "主键列没有映射");
        let no_pk = Table { primary_key: None, ..orders() };
        let r = d.import_statements("s", &no_pk, &names(&["id"]), &one, WriteMode::Upsert);
        assert!(matches!(r, Err(Error::Invalid { .. })), "没有主键");
        assert!(
            matches!(err(&["id", "name"], &one, WriteMode::Append), Error::Invalid { .. }),
            "值的个数不对"
        );
        // 没有行时没有语句
        assert!(d.import_statements("s", &t, &names(&["id"]), &[], WriteMode::Append).unwrap().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn interval_and_unknown_types_are_cast_from_text_on_postgres() {
        let mut t = orders();
        let typed = |name: &str, native: &str, logical: LogicalType| Column {
            data_type: ColumnType { native: native.into(), logical: logical.clone() },
            ..col(name, logical)
        };
        t.columns.push(typed("span", "interval", LogicalType::Interval));
        t.columns.push(typed("feel", "mood", LogicalType::Other));
        let row = vec![Value::String("1 day".into()), Value::String("happy".into())];
        let st = Dialect::new(Variant::Postgres)
            .import_statements("s", &t, &names(&["span", "feel"]), &[row], WriteMode::Append)
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            st[0].sql,
            "INSERT INTO s.orders (span, feel) VALUES ($1::text::interval, $2::text::mood)"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn create_table_and_delete_all() {
        let mut id = col("id", LogicalType::Int { bits: 64 });
        id.nullable = false;
        id.data_type.native = "BIGINT".into();
        let mut name = col("名称", LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(100) });
        name.data_type.native = "VARCHAR(100)".into();
        let t = Table {
            name: "导入表".into(),
            columns: vec![id, name],
            primary_key: Some(PrimaryKey { name: None, columns: vec!["id".into()] }),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            Dialect::new(Variant::Postgres).create_table_sql("public", &t),
            "CREATE TABLE public.\"导入表\" (\n    id BIGINT NOT NULL,\n    \"名称\" VARCHAR(100),\n    PRIMARY KEY (id)\n)"
        );
        assert_eq!(Dialect::new(Variant::MySql).delete_all_sql("shop", "orders"), "DELETE FROM shop.orders");
        assert_eq!(
            Dialect::new(Variant::Oracle).delete_all_sql("HR", "orders"),
            r#"DELETE FROM HR."orders""#
        );
    }
}
```

### Task 4: 服务层：读取导入文件、预览、类型推断与值转换

新增依赖 `calamine` 0.37.0（特性 `chrono`）、`encoding_rs` 0.8.42、`encoding_rs_io` 0.1.8（工作区和 `dblens-service`）。`Cargo.lock` 由 cargo 自动更新。

`dblens-service/src/import/`：
- `file.rs`：`FileReader`（`open`、`columns`、`next_row`、`progress`、`warnings`）。
  - CSV：编码自动判断（BOM 优先；开头 64 KB 能按 UTF-8 解码就用 UTF-8，否则 GB18030），`encoding_rs_io` 边读边转码；分隔符自动判断（前 20 行中每行出现次数相同且最多，引号内的不算；`.tsv` 为 Tab）；列数不齐时多出的忽略、缺少的为 NULL；
  - Excel：calamine，单元格按文件中的类型（0 点的日期时间当作日期），错误值为 NULL 并计入警告；
  - 列名去空白、空的为 `column_n`、重名加 `_2`；
  - `open_text` 也给 SQL 文件用（任务 5）。
- `values.rs`：`infer_type`（布尔 → 64 位整数 → 精确小数（精度至少 10、超过 38 用字符串）→ 日期 → 日期时间 → 字符串（长度 × 2，至少 50，超过 4000 用长文本），不推断浮点）；`convert`（按目标列的逻辑类型转换，文本格式与 M2a 前端相同；带时区的类型没写时区时按本机时区；"是""否"写成转义，源码检查不把它当界面文字）。
- `mod.rs`：`preview_file`（前 100 行，按前 1000 行推断类型）。测试也在这里（源码检查只跳过 `#[cfg(test)]` 之后的内容）。

`export/mod.rs` 让 `cell_text` 在 crate 内可用。后端词条加 `import.read_failed`、`import.bad_delimiter`、`import.no_sheet`、`import.bad_value`。

**Files:**
- Modify: `Cargo.toml`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-core/locales/app.json`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/Cargo.toml`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/export/mod.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-service/src/import/file.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-service/src/import/mod.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-service/src/import/values.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：任务 3 无；用到 `crate::export::cell_text`。
- Produces（`dblens_service`）：
  - `FileOptions { format: FileFormat, encoding: TextEncoding, delimiter: Option<char>, header: bool, sheet: Option<String> }`（Deserialize；`FileOptions::csv()`）；
  - `FileReader::open(&Path, name: &str, &FileOptions) -> Result<FileReader>`、`next_row(&mut self) -> Result<Option<FileRow>>`、`FileRow { line: u64, values: Vec<Value> }`；
  - `FilePreview { columns, rows, types, warnings: ReadWarnings, sheets, encoding, delimiter }`、`preview_file(&Path, &str, &FileOptions) -> Result<FilePreview>`；
  - `infer_type(values) -> LogicalType`、`convert(&Value, &Column, EmptyAs) -> Result<Value>`、`EmptyAs::{Null, EmptyString}`；
  - crate 内：`open_text(&Path, TextEncoding) -> Result<TextFile>`、`text(&Value) -> String`。

- [ ] **Step 5: 新建 `crates/dblens-service/src/import/file.rs`**

```rust
//! 读取导入文件（M2b 设计文档 4.2–4.4 节）：CSV 流式读取（自动判断编码和分隔符），Excel 用 calamine。
//! 单元格统一用 `Value`：CSV 的单元格是文本（空的为 NULL），Excel 的单元格按文件中的类型。

use std::fs::File;
use std::io::{BufReader, Read};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

use calamine::{Data, Range, Reader, open_workbook_auto};
use chrono::NaiveTime;
use dblens_core::{Error, Result, Value, msg};
use encoding_rs::{Encoding, GB18030, UTF_8, UTF_16BE, UTF_16LE};
use encoding_rs_io::DecodeReaderBytesBuilder;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// 判断编码时读取的字节数。
const SNIFF_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
/// 判断分隔符时看的行数。
const SNIFF_LINES: usize = 20;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum FileFormat {
    Csv,
    Excel,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TextEncoding {
    #[default]
    Auto,
    Utf8,
    Gbk,
}

/// 文件的读取选项。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
pub struct FileOptions {
    pub format: FileFormat,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub encoding: TextEncoding,
    /// CSV 的分隔符；不给时自动判断（`.tsv` 文件为 Tab）。
    #[serde(default)]
    pub delimiter: Option<char>,
    /// 首行是列名。
    #[serde(default = "yes")]
    pub header: bool,
    /// Excel 的工作表；不给时取第一个。
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sheet: Option<String>,
}

fn yes() -> bool {
    true
}

impl FileOptions {
    pub fn csv() -> Self {
        Self {
            format: FileFormat::Csv,
            encoding: TextEncoding::Auto,
            delimiter: None,
            header: true,
            sheet: None,
        }
    }
}

/// 读取中的警告计数。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ReadWarnings {
    /// 列数与列名行不一致的行（多出的列忽略，缺少的列为 NULL）。
    pub ragged_rows: u64,
    /// 转码失败、替换成 `�` 的单元格。
    pub bad_encoding: u64,
    /// Excel 的错误值（`#N/A` 等），按 NULL 读取。
    pub error_cells: u64,
}

/// 文件中的一行。`line` 为行号：CSV 按记录计（首行为第 1 行），Excel 为工作表的行号。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct FileRow {
    pub line: u64,
    pub values: Vec<Value>,
}

/// 统计读过的字节数（进度用）。
struct Counting<R> {
    inner: R,
    read: Arc<AtomicU64>,
}

impl<R: Read> Read for Counting<R> {
    fn read(&mut self, buf: &mut [u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
        let n = self.inner.read(buf)?;
        self.read.fetch_add(n as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
        Ok(n)
    }
}

type CsvReader = csv::Reader<Box<dyn Read + Send>>;

enum Inner {
    Csv { reader: CsvReader, record: csv::StringRecord, line: u64 },
    Excel { range: Range<Data>, next: usize },
}

/// 打开的导入文件：已读过列名行（或按首行的列数生成了列名）。
pub struct FileReader {
    columns: Vec<String>,
    inner: Inner,
    /// 首行不是列名时，先交出读到的首行。
    pending: Option<FileRow>,
    bytes: Arc<AtomicU64>,
    total: u64,
    warnings: ReadWarnings,
    /// 实际使用的编码和分隔符（CSV）。
    pub encoding: Option<&'static str>,
    pub delimiter: Option<char>,
    /// Excel 的全部工作表名。
    pub sheets: Vec<String>,
}

fn read_error(e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> Error {
    Error::invalid(msg!("import.read_failed", error = e))
}

/// 判断编码：有 BOM 按 BOM；否则开头能按 UTF-8 解码（末尾被截断的多字节字符不算错）就用 UTF-8，不能就用 GB18030。
pub fn detect_encoding(head: &[u8]) -> &'static Encoding {
    if let Some((enc, _)) = Encoding::for_bom(head) {
        return enc;
    }
    match std::str::from_utf8(head) {
        Ok(_) => UTF_8,
        Err(e) if e.error_len().is_none() => UTF_8,
        Err(_) => GB18030,
    }
}

/// 判断分隔符：前 20 行中（引号内的不算）每行出现次数相同且最多的候选字符；都不满足时用逗号。
pub fn detect_delimiter(sample: &str) -> char {
    let lines: Vec<&str> = sample.lines().filter(|l| !l.trim().is_empty()).take(SNIFF_LINES).collect();
    // 最后一行可能被截断，有多行时不用它
    let lines =
        if lines.len() > 1 && !sample.ends_with('\n') { &lines[..lines.len() - 1] } else { &lines[..] };
    let mut best = (',', 0);
    for d in [',', ';', '\t', '|'] {
        let counts: Vec<usize> = lines
            .iter()
            .map(|l| {
                let mut quoted = false;
                l.chars()
                    .filter(|&c| {
                        if c == '"' {
                            quoted = !quoted;
                        }
                        c == d && !quoted
                    })
                    .count()
            })
            .collect();
        if let Some(&first) = counts.first()
            && first > 0
            && counts.iter().all(|&n| n == first)
            && first > best.1
        {
            best = (d, first);
        }
    }
    best.0
}

/// 列名：去掉首尾空白，空的改为 `column_n`，重名的加后缀 `_2`、`_3`。
pub fn clean_names(raw: impl IntoIterator<Item = String>) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut out: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    for (k, name) in raw.into_iter().enumerate() {
        let base = match name.trim() {
            "" => format!("column_{}", k + 1),
            t => t.to_string(),
        };
        let mut name = base.clone();
        let mut n = 2;
        while out.iter().any(|o| o.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&name)) {
            name = format!("{base}_{n}");
            n += 1;
        }
        out.push(name);
    }
    out
}

/// Excel 单元格 → 值。日期时间的时间为 0 点时当作日期。
pub(crate) fn excel_value(d: &Data, warnings: &mut ReadWarnings) -> Value {
    match d {
        Data::Empty => Value::Null,
        Data::Int(n) => Value::Int(*n),
        Data::Float(f) => Value::Float(*f),
        Data::String(s) if s.is_empty() => Value::Null,
        Data::String(s) => Value::String(s.clone()),
        Data::Bool(b) => Value::Bool(*b),
        Data::DateTime(dt) => match dt.as_datetime() {
            Some(t) if t.time() == NaiveTime::MIN => Value::Date(t.date()),
            Some(t) => Value::DateTime(t),
            None => Value::String(dt.to_string()),
        },
        Data::DateTimeIso(s) | Data::DurationIso(s) => Value::String(s.clone()),
        Data::Error(_) => {
            warnings.error_cells += 1;
            Value::Null
        }
    }
}

impl FileReader {
    /// 打开文件，读取列名。`name` 是原文件名（判断 `.tsv` 用）。
    pub fn open(path: &Path, name: &str, opts: &FileOptions) -> Result<Self> {
        match opts.format {
            FileFormat::Csv => Self::open_csv(path, name, opts),
            FileFormat::Excel => Self::open_excel(path, opts),
        }
    }

    fn open_csv(path: &Path, name: &str, opts: &FileOptions) -> Result<Self> {
        let mut file = File::open(path).map_err(read_error)?;
        let total = file.metadata().map_err(read_error)?.len();
        let mut head = vec![0u8; SNIFF_BYTES];
        let mut n = 0;
        while n < head.len() {
            match file.read(&mut head[n..]).map_err(read_error)? {
                0 => break,
                k => n += k,
            }
        }
        head.truncate(n);
        let encoding = match opts.encoding {
            TextEncoding::Auto => detect_encoding(&head),
            TextEncoding::Utf8 => UTF_8,
            TextEncoding::Gbk => GB18030,
        };
        let delimiter = match opts.delimiter {
            Some(d) => d,
            None if name.to_ascii_lowercase().ends_with(".tsv") => '\t',
            None => {
                let (sample, _, _) = encoding.decode(&head);
                detect_delimiter(&sample)
            }
        };
        if !delimiter.is_ascii() {
            return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.bad_delimiter", delimiter = delimiter)));
        }
        let bytes = Arc::new(AtomicU64::new(0));
        let raw = Counting { inner: std::io::Cursor::new(head).chain(file), read: bytes.clone() };
        // BOM 优先于选择的编码；输出为 UTF-8，无法转码的字节替换为 U+FFFD
        let decoded = DecodeReaderBytesBuilder::new().encoding(Some(encoding)).bom_override(true).build(raw);
        let reader: Box<dyn Read + Send> = Box::new(BufReader::new(decoded));
        let reader = csv::ReaderBuilder::new()
            .has_headers(false)
            .flexible(true)
            .delimiter(delimiter as u8)
            .from_reader(reader);
        let label = match encoding {
            e if e == UTF_16LE || e == UTF_16BE => "UTF-16",
            e if e == GB18030 => "GBK",
            _ => "UTF-8",
        };
        let mut me = Self {
            columns: Vec::new(),
            inner: Inner::Csv { reader, record: csv::StringRecord::new(), line: 0 },
            pending: None,
            bytes,
            total,
            warnings: ReadWarnings::default(),
            encoding: Some(label),
            delimiter: Some(delimiter),
            sheets: Vec::new(),
        };
        me.read_header(opts.header)?;
        Ok(me)
    }

    fn open_excel(path: &Path, opts: &FileOptions) -> Result<Self> {
        let mut book = open_workbook_auto(path).map_err(read_error)?;
        let sheets = book.sheet_names();
        let sheet = match &opts.sheet {
            Some(s) if sheets.contains(s) => s.clone(),
            Some(s) => return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.no_sheet", sheet = s))),
            None => {
                sheets.first().cloned().ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid(msg!("import.no_sheet", sheet = "")))?
            }
        };
        let range = book.worksheet_range(&sheet).map_err(read_error)?;
        let total = range.height() as u64;
        let mut me = Self {
            columns: Vec::new(),
            inner: Inner::Excel { range, next: 0 },
            pending: None,
            bytes: Arc::new(AtomicU64::new(0)),
            total,
            warnings: ReadWarnings::default(),
            encoding: None,
            delimiter: None,
            sheets,
        };
        me.read_header(opts.header)?;
        Ok(me)
    }

    /// 首行是列名时读作列名；否则按首行的列数生成 `column_n`，首行留作数据。
    fn read_header(&mut self, header: bool) -> Result<()> {
        let Some(first) = self.raw_row()? else { return Ok(()) };
        if header {
            let names = first.values.iter().map(|v| match v {
                Value::Null => String::new(),
                Value::String(s) => s.clone(),
                other => super::text(other),
            });
            self.columns = clean_names(names);
        } else {
            self.columns = clean_names(vec![String::new(); first.values.len()]);
            self.pending = Some(first);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 读一行原样的值（列数不整理）。
    fn raw_row(&mut self) -> Result<Option<FileRow>> {
        match &mut self.inner {
            Inner::Csv { reader, record, line } => {
                if !reader.read_record(record).map_err(read_error)? {
                    return Ok(None);
                }
                *line += 1;
                let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(record.len());
                for field in record.iter() {
                    if field.contains('\u{fffd}') {
                        self.warnings.bad_encoding += 1;
                    }
                    values.push(if field.is_empty() {
                        Value::Null
                    } else {
                        Value::String(field.to_string())
                    });
                }
                Ok(Some(FileRow { line: *line, values }))
            }
            Inner::Excel { range, next } => {
                let width = range.width();
                let Some(row) = range.rows().nth(*next) else { return Ok(None) };
                let line = range.start().map_or(0, |(r, _)| r as u64) + *next as u64 + 1;
                *next += 1;
                let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(width);
                for d in row {
                    values.push(excel_value(d, &mut self.warnings));
                }
                Ok(Some(FileRow { line, values }))
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn columns(&self) -> &[String] {
        &self.columns
    }

    /// 下一行数据，值的个数与列数相同（多出的忽略，缺少的为 NULL，计入警告）。
    pub fn next_row(&mut self) -> Result<Option<FileRow>> {
        let row = match self.pending.take() {
            Some(r) => Some(r),
            None => self.raw_row()?,
        };
        let Some(mut row) = row else { return Ok(None) };
        let width = self.columns.len();
        if row.values.len() != width {
            // Excel 的已用区域是矩形，行尾的空单元格不算列数不齐
            if matches!(self.inner, Inner::Csv { .. }) {
                self.warnings.ragged_rows += 1;
            }
            row.values.resize(width, Value::Null);
        }
        Ok(Some(row))
    }

    /// 进度：CSV 为已读字节和文件大小，Excel 为已读行数和总行数。
    pub fn progress(&self) -> (u64, u64) {
        match &self.inner {
            Inner::Csv { .. } => (self.bytes.load(Ordering::Relaxed).min(self.total), self.total),
            Inner::Excel { next, .. } => (*next as u64, self.total),
        }
    }

    pub fn warnings(&self) -> ReadWarnings {
        self.warnings
    }
}
```

```rust
//! 导入（M2b 设计文档第 4、5 节）：读取 CSV / Excel 文件、预览与类型推断、值的转换。

mod file;
mod values;

use std::path::Path;

use dblens_core::{LogicalType, Result, Value};
use serde::Serialize;

use crate::export::{ExportWarnings, cell_text};

pub use file::{FileFormat, FileOptions, FileReader, FileRow, ReadWarnings, TextEncoding};
pub use values::{EmptyAs, INFER_ROWS, convert, infer_type};

/// 预览返回的行数。
pub const PREVIEW_ROWS: usize = 100;

/// 值的文本（与导出、结果表格的显示一致）；NULL 为空字符串。
pub(crate) fn text(v: &Value) -> String {
    cell_text(v, &mut ExportWarnings::default()).unwrap_or_default()
}

/// 文件预览：列名、前 100 行、按前 1000 行推断的类型。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct FilePreview {
    pub columns: Vec<String>,
    pub rows: Vec<Vec<Value>>,
    pub types: Vec<LogicalType>,
    pub warnings: ReadWarnings,
    /// Excel 的全部工作表名。
    pub sheets: Vec<String>,
    /// CSV 实际使用的编码（`UTF-8`、`GBK`、`UTF-16`）和分隔符。
    pub encoding: Option<&'static str>,
    pub delimiter: Option<char>,
}

/// 读取文件开头生成预览。读取文件是阻塞操作，异步代码中要放在 `spawn_blocking` 里调用。
pub fn preview_file(path: &Path, name: &str, opts: &FileOptions) -> Result<FilePreview> {
    let mut reader = FileReader::open(path, name, opts)?;
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    while rows.len() < INFER_ROWS {
        match reader.next_row()? {
            Some(r) => rows.push(r.values),
            None => break,
        }
    }
    let types = (0..reader.columns().len()).map(|k| infer_type(rows.iter().map(|r| &r[k]))).collect();
    rows.truncate(PREVIEW_ROWS);
    Ok(FilePreview {
        columns: reader.columns().to_vec(),
        rows,
        types,
        warnings: reader.warnings(),
        sheets: reader.sheets.clone(),
        encoding: reader.encoding,
        delimiter: reader.delimiter,
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    use chrono::{NaiveDate, NaiveDateTime};
    use dblens_core::schema::Column;
    use dblens_core::{ColumnType, Error, LogicalType, Value};
    use encoding_rs::GB18030;

    use super::file::{detect_delimiter, detect_encoding};
    use super::*;

    fn write(dir: &tempfile::TempDir, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> PathBuf {
        let p = dir.path().join(name);
        std::fs::write(&p, bytes).unwrap();
        p
    }

    fn s(v: &str) -> Value {
        Value::String(v.into())
    }

    fn read_all(
        path: &std::path::Path,
        name: &str,
        opts: &FileOptions,
    ) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<FileRow>, ReadWarnings) {
        let mut r = FileReader::open(path, name, opts).unwrap();
        let mut rows = Vec::new();
        while let Some(row) = r.next_row().unwrap() {
            rows.push(row);
        }
        (r.columns().to_vec(), rows, r.warnings())
    }

    #[test]
    fn csv_utf8_with_bom_quotes_and_ragged_rows() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let text = "\u{feff}编号,名称, 备注 ,名称\n1,\"含,逗号\",\"多\n行\",x\n2,b\n3,c,,y,多余\n";
        let p = write(&dir, "a.csv", text.as_bytes());
        let (cols, rows, w) = read_all(&p, "a.csv", &FileOptions::csv());
        assert_eq!(cols, ["编号", "名称", "备注", "名称_2"]);
        assert_eq!(rows.len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(rows[0].values, [s("1"), s("含,逗号"), s("多\n行"), s("x")]);
        // 行号按记录计，列名行为第 1 行
        assert_eq!(rows.iter().map(|r| r.line).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [2, 3, 4]);
        // 缺少的列为 NULL，多出的忽略；空单元格为 NULL
        assert_eq!(rows[1].values, [s("2"), s("b"), Value::Null, Value::Null]);
        assert_eq!(rows[2].values, [s("3"), s("c"), Value::Null, s("y")]);
        assert_eq!(w.ragged_rows, 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn csv_detects_gbk_and_delimiters() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let (gbk, _, _) = GB18030.encode("名称;数量\n苹果;3\n香蕉;5\n");
        let p = write(&dir, "gbk.csv", &gbk);
        let preview = preview_file(&p, "gbk.csv", &FileOptions::csv()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((preview.encoding, preview.delimiter), (Some("GBK"), Some(';')));
        assert_eq!(preview.columns, ["名称", "数量"]);
        assert_eq!(preview.rows[0], [s("苹果"), s("3")]);
        // 指定编码时不再判断
        let utf8 = FileOptions { encoding: TextEncoding::Utf8, ..FileOptions::csv() };
        let wrong = preview_file(&p, "gbk.csv", &utf8).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(wrong.encoding, Some("UTF-8"));
        assert!(wrong.warnings.bad_encoding > 0, "按 UTF-8 读 GBK 文件会出现替换字符");
        // .tsv 默认 Tab；判断分隔符时引号内的不算
        let p = write(&dir, "t.tsv", "a,b\tc\n1,2\t3\n".as_bytes());
        assert_eq!(preview_file(&p, "t.tsv", &FileOptions::csv()).unwrap().columns, ["a,b", "c"]);
        assert_eq!(detect_delimiter("a|b|c\n1|2|3\n"), '|');
        assert_eq!(detect_delimiter("\"x,y\";b\n1;2\n"), ';');
        assert_eq!(detect_delimiter("单列\n值\n"), ',');
        assert_eq!(detect_encoding(&[0xe4, 0xb8]), encoding_rs::UTF_8, "末尾被截断的多字节字符不算错");
    }

    #[test]
    fn csv_without_header_keeps_first_row() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let p = write(&dir, "n.csv", b"1,a\n2,b\n");
        let opts = FileOptions { header: false, ..FileOptions::csv() };
        let (cols, rows, _) = read_all(&p, "n.csv", &opts);
        assert_eq!(cols, ["column_1", "column_2"]);
        assert_eq!(rows.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(rows[0].line, 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn excel_cells_keep_their_types() {
        use rust_xlsxwriter::{ExcelDateTime, Format, Workbook};
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("b.xlsx");
        let mut book = Workbook::new();
        let sheet = book.add_worksheet().set_name("数据").unwrap();
        let date = Format::new().set_num_format("yyyy-mm-dd");
        let datetime = Format::new().set_num_format("yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm");
        for (c, h) in ["数量", "价格", "日期", "时间", "有效", "名称"].iter().enumerate() {
            sheet.write_string(0, c as u16, *h).unwrap();
        }
        sheet.write_number(1, 0, 3).unwrap();
        sheet.write_number(1, 1, 1.25).unwrap();
        let day = ExcelDateTime::from_ymd(2026, 10, 27).unwrap();
        sheet.write_datetime_with_format(1, 2, day, &date).unwrap();
        let dt = ExcelDateTime::from_ymd(2026, 10, 27).unwrap().and_hms(8, 30, 0).unwrap();
        sheet.write_datetime_with_format(1, 3, &dt, &datetime).unwrap();
        sheet.write_boolean(1, 4, true).unwrap();
        sheet.write_string(1, 5, "苹果").unwrap();
        sheet.write_number(2, 0, 4).unwrap();
        book.add_worksheet().set_name("另一张").unwrap();
        book.save(&path).unwrap();

        let opts = FileOptions { format: FileFormat::Excel, ..FileOptions::csv() };
        let preview = preview_file(&path, "b.xlsx", &opts).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(preview.sheets, ["数据", "另一张"]);
        assert_eq!(preview.columns, ["数量", "价格", "日期", "时间", "有效", "名称"]);
        assert_eq!(
            preview.rows[0],
            [
                Value::Float(3.0),
                Value::Float(1.25),
                Value::Date(NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(2026, 10, 27).unwrap()),
                Value::DateTime(
                    NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(2026, 10, 27).unwrap().and_hms_opt(8, 30, 0).unwrap()
                ),
                Value::Bool(true),
                s("苹果"),
            ]
        );
        // 已用区域是矩形：第 3 行的空单元格为 NULL，不算列数不齐
        assert_eq!(preview.rows[1][1], Value::Null);
        assert_eq!(preview.warnings.ragged_rows, 0);
        assert_eq!(
            preview.types,
            [
                LogicalType::Int { bits: 64 },
                LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some(10), scale: Some(2) },
                LogicalType::Date,
                LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: false, precision: None },
                LogicalType::Bool,
                LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(50) },
            ]
        );
        let missing = FileOptions { sheet: Some("没有".into()), ..opts };
        assert!(matches!(FileReader::open(&path, "b.xlsx", &missing), Err(Error::Invalid { .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn excel_error_cells_read_as_null() {
        let mut w = ReadWarnings::default();
        let v = super::file::excel_value(&calamine::Data::Error(calamine::CellErrorType::NA), &mut w);
        assert_eq!((v, w.error_cells), (Value::Null, 1));
    }

    #[test]
    fn infers_types_in_order() {
        let t = |vals: &[&str]| {
            infer_type(
                vals.iter().map(|v| if v.is_empty() { Value::Null } else { s(v) }).collect::<Vec<_>>().iter(),
            )
        };
        assert_eq!(t(&["true", "FALSE", ""]), LogicalType::Bool);
        assert_eq!(t(&["1", "-20", "+3"]), LogicalType::Int { bits: 64 });
        assert_eq!(t(&["1", "2.5", "-30.125"]), LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some(10), scale: Some(3) });
        let long = format!("1{}.5", "0".repeat(30));
        assert_eq!(t(&[&long]), LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some(32), scale: Some(1) });
        assert_eq!(
            t(&[&format!("1{}", "0".repeat(40))]),
            LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(82) },
            "超过 38 位用字符串"
        );
        assert_eq!(t(&["1e5"]), LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(50) }, "带指数的不推断为数字");
        assert_eq!(t(&["2026-10-27", "2026/1/2"]), LogicalType::Date);
        assert_eq!(
            t(&["2026-10-27", "2026-10-27 08:30"]),
            LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: false, precision: None }
        );
        assert_eq!(t(&["a", "数洞数洞"]), LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(50) });
        assert_eq!(t(&[&"字".repeat(30)]), LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(60) });
        assert_eq!(t(&[&"x".repeat(2001)]), LogicalType::Text);
        assert_eq!(t(&["", ""]), LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(50) }, "全空的列");
    }

    fn column(name: &str, native: &str, logical: LogicalType) -> Column {
        Column {
            name: name.into(),
            ordinal: 1,
            data_type: ColumnType { native: native.into(), logical },
            nullable: true,
            default: None,
            comment: None,
            auto_increment: false,
            generated: None,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn converts_values_by_column_type() {
        let c = |l: LogicalType| column("c", "t", l);
        let ok = |v: Value, l: LogicalType| convert(&v, &c(l), EmptyAs::Null).unwrap();
        let int32 = LogicalType::Int { bits: 32 };
        assert_eq!(ok(s(" 42 "), int32.clone()), Value::Int(42));
        assert_eq!(ok(Value::Float(7.0), int32.clone()), Value::Int(7), "Excel 的数字");
        assert!(convert(&s("3000000000"), &c(int32.clone()), EmptyAs::Null).is_err(), "超出 32 位");
        assert!(convert(&s("1.5"), &c(int32), EmptyAs::Null).is_err());
        assert_eq!(
            ok(s("18446744073709551615"), LogicalType::UInt { bits: 64 }),
            Value::Decimal("18446744073709551615".into())
        );
        let dec = LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some(10), scale: Some(2) };
        assert_eq!(ok(s("12.30"), dec.clone()), Value::Decimal("12.30".into()), "不经过浮点");
        assert_eq!(ok(Value::Float(0.1), dec.clone()), Value::Decimal("0.1".into()));
        assert!(convert(&s("12,30"), &c(dec), EmptyAs::Null).is_err());
        assert_eq!(ok(s("是"), LogicalType::Bool), Value::Bool(true));
        assert_eq!(ok(s("0"), LogicalType::Bool), Value::Bool(false));
        let d = NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(2026, 1, 2).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ok(s("2026/1/2"), LogicalType::Date), Value::Date(d));
        let ts = LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: false, precision: None };
        let at = |h, m, sec| NaiveDateTime::new(d, chrono::NaiveTime::from_hms_opt(h, m, sec).unwrap());
        assert_eq!(ok(s("2026-01-02T08:30"), ts.clone()), Value::DateTime(at(8, 30, 0)));
        assert_eq!(ok(Value::Date(d), ts), Value::DateTime(at(0, 0, 0)));
        let tz = LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: true, precision: None };
        match ok(s("2026-01-02 08:30:00+08:00"), tz.clone()) {
            Value::DateTimeTz(t) => assert_eq!(t.to_rfc3339(), "2026-01-02T08:30:00+08:00"),
            v => panic!("{v:?}"),
        }
        match ok(s("2026-01-02 08:30:00Z"), tz.clone()) {
            Value::DateTimeTz(t) => assert_eq!(t.offset().local_minus_utc(), 0),
            v => panic!("{v:?}"),
        }
        assert!(matches!(ok(s("2026-01-02 08:30"), tz), Value::DateTimeTz(_)), "没有时区按本地时区");
        assert_eq!(ok(s("0x0aFF"), LogicalType::Blob), Value::Bytes(vec![0x0a, 0xff]));
        assert_eq!(ok(s("{\"a\": 1}"), LogicalType::Json), Value::Json(serde_json::json!({"a": 1})));
        assert_eq!(ok(Value::Float(1.5), LogicalType::Text), s("1.5"), "数字写入文本列");
        assert_eq!(ok(s("1 day"), LogicalType::Interval), s("1 day"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_cells_and_error_messages() {
        let text = column("备注", "VARCHAR(20)", LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(20) });
        let num = column("数量", "INTEGER", LogicalType::Int { bits: 32 });
        assert_eq!(convert(&Value::Null, &text, EmptyAs::Null).unwrap(), Value::Null);
        assert_eq!(convert(&Value::Null, &text, EmptyAs::EmptyString).unwrap(), s(""));
        assert_eq!(
            convert(&Value::Null, &num, EmptyAs::EmptyString).unwrap(),
            Value::Null,
            "非文本列仍为 NULL"
        );
        let err = convert(&s("abc"), &num, EmptyAs::Null).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(err.contains("数量") && err.contains("abc") && err.contains("INTEGER"), "{err}");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 新建 `crates/dblens-service/src/import/values.rs`**

```rust
//! 导入的值（M2b 设计文档 4.5、4.6 节）：按文件内容推断新建表的列类型；按目标列的类型转换文件中的值。
//! 文本格式与表数据编辑（M2a 前端 `parse.ts`）一致。

use chrono::{DateTime, FixedOffset, Local, NaiveDate, NaiveDateTime, NaiveTime, TimeZone};
use dblens_core::schema::Column;
use dblens_core::{Error, LogicalType, Result, Value, msg};

use super::text;

/// 推断时最多看的行数。
pub const INFER_ROWS: usize = 1000;

/// 布尔值的中文写法"是""否"：是可以输入的文字，不是给用户看的，用转义写（源码检查不把它当作界面文字）。
const YES: &str = "\u{662f}";
const NO: &str = "\u{5426}";

fn parse_date(s: &str) -> Option<NaiveDate> {
    ["%Y-%m-%d", "%Y/%m/%d"].iter().find_map(|f| NaiveDate::parse_from_str(s, f).ok())
}

fn parse_time(s: &str) -> Option<NaiveTime> {
    ["%H:%M:%S%.f", "%H:%M"].iter().find_map(|f| NaiveTime::parse_from_str(s, f).ok())
}

/// 日期加空格或 `T` 加时间。
fn parse_datetime(s: &str) -> Option<NaiveDateTime> {
    let (d, t) = s.split_once([' ', 'T'])?;
    Some(parse_date(d)?.and_time(parse_time(t.trim())?))
}

/// 带时区的日期时间：末尾可以有 `Z`、`±HH:MM`、`±HHMM` 或 `±HH`；没有时按本地时区。
fn parse_datetime_tz(s: &str) -> Option<DateTime<FixedOffset>> {
    let (body, offset) = match s.strip_suffix('Z') {
        Some(body) => (body, Some(0)),
        None => split_offset(s),
    };
    let naive = parse_datetime(body.trim_end())
        .or_else(|| parse_date(body.trim_end()).map(|d| d.and_time(NaiveTime::MIN)))?;
    match offset {
        Some(secs) => FixedOffset::east_opt(secs)?.from_local_datetime(&naive).single(),
        None => Local.from_local_datetime(&naive).earliest().map(|t| t.fixed_offset()),
    }
}

/// 拆出末尾的时区偏移（秒）。时区必须跟在时间后面，日期中的 `-` 不算。
fn split_offset(s: &str) -> (&str, Option<i32>) {
    let Some(k) = s.rfind(['+', '-']) else { return (s, None) };
    let (body, off) = s.split_at(k);
    let digits: String = off[1..].chars().filter(|c| *c != ':').collect();
    if !body.contains(':') || !matches!(digits.len(), 2 | 4) || !digits.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
        return (s, None);
    }
    let h: i32 = digits[..2].parse().unwrap_or(0);
    let m: i32 = digits.get(2..).map_or(0, |m| m.parse().unwrap_or(0));
    let sign = if off.starts_with('-') { -1 } else { 1 };
    (body, Some(sign * (h * 3600 + m * 60)))
}

/// 精确小数的文本（与表数据编辑相同：可带符号、小数点和指数）。
fn is_decimal(s: &str) -> bool {
    let s = s.strip_prefix(['+', '-']).unwrap_or(s);
    let (mantissa, exp) = match s.split_once(['e', 'E']) {
        Some((m, e)) => (m, Some(e)),
        None => (s, None),
    };
    let (int, frac) = mantissa.split_once('.').unwrap_or((mantissa, ""));
    let digits = |t: &str| t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit());
    let mantissa_ok = (!int.is_empty() || !frac.is_empty()) && digits(int) && digits(frac);
    let exp_ok = exp.is_none_or(|e| {
        let e = e.strip_prefix(['+', '-']).unwrap_or(e);
        !e.is_empty() && digits(e)
    });
    mantissa_ok && exp_ok
}

/// 推断用：一个值能归入哪些类型。
struct Fits {
    bool: bool,
    int: bool,
    /// 精确小数（无指数）：整数部分位数、小数位数。
    decimal: Option<(u32, u32)>,
    date: bool,
    datetime: bool,
}

fn fits(v: &Value) -> Fits {
    let none = Fits { bool: false, int: false, decimal: None, date: false, datetime: false };
    let plain_decimal = |s: &str| -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
        let s = s.strip_prefix(['+', '-']).unwrap_or(s);
        let (int, frac) = s.split_once('.').unwrap_or((s, ""));
        let ok = !int.is_empty()
            && int.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())
            && frac.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit());
        let int = int.trim_start_matches('0');
        ok.then_some((int.len().max(1) as u32, frac.len() as u32))
    };
    match v {
        Value::Bool(_) => Fits { bool: true, ..none },
        Value::Int(n) => Fits { int: true, decimal: plain_decimal(&n.to_string()), ..none },
        Value::Float(f) => {
            let s = f.to_string();
            Fits { int: s.parse::<i64>().is_ok(), decimal: plain_decimal(&s), ..none }
        }
        Value::Date(_) => Fits { date: true, datetime: true, ..none },
        Value::DateTime(_) => Fits { datetime: true, ..none },
        Value::String(s) => {
            let s = s.trim();
            let date = parse_date(s).is_some();
            Fits {
                bool: s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("true") || s.eq_ignore_ascii_case("false"),
                int: s.parse::<i64>().is_ok(),
                decimal: plain_decimal(s),
                date,
                datetime: date || parse_datetime(s).is_some(),
            }
        }
        _ => none,
    }
}

/// 按一列的值推断类型（空值不参与）：布尔 → 64 位整数 → 精确小数 → 日期 → 日期时间 → 字符串。
/// 不推断浮点；全空的列为 VarChar(50)。
pub fn infer_type<'a>(values: impl IntoIterator<Item = &'a Value>) -> LogicalType {
    let (mut bool, mut int, mut decimal, mut date, mut datetime) =
        (true, true, Some((0u32, 0u32)), true, true);
    let mut max_len = 0usize;
    let mut any = false;
    for v in values {
        if matches!(v, Value::Null) {
            continue;
        }
        any = true;
        let f = fits(v);
        bool &= f.bool;
        int &= f.int;
        decimal = match (decimal, f.decimal) {
            (Some((i, s)), Some((fi, fs))) => Some((i.max(fi), s.max(fs))),
            _ => None,
        };
        date &= f.date;
        datetime &= f.datetime;
        max_len = max_len.max(text(v).chars().count());
    }
    if !any {
        return LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(50) };
    }
    if bool {
        return LogicalType::Bool;
    }
    if int {
        return LogicalType::Int { bits: 64 };
    }
    if let Some((i, s)) = decimal
        && i + s <= 38
    {
        return LogicalType::Decimal { precision: Some((i + s).max(10)), scale: Some(s as i32) };
    }
    if date {
        return LogicalType::Date;
    }
    if datetime {
        return LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: false, precision: None };
    }
    match (max_len * 2).max(50) {
        n if n > 4000 => LogicalType::Text,
        n => LogicalType::VarChar { len: Some(n as u32) },
    }
}

/// 空单元格的写法。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum EmptyAs {
    Null,
    /// 文本列写空字符串（其他类型仍为 NULL）。
    EmptyString,
}

fn is_text(t: &LogicalType) -> bool {
    matches!(t, LogicalType::Char { .. } | LogicalType::VarChar { .. } | LogicalType::Text)
}

/// 把文件中的值转换成目标列的类型。失败时的错误包含列名和原值。
pub fn convert(v: &Value, col: &Column, empty: EmptyAs) -> Result<Value> {
    let logical = &col.data_type.logical;
    if matches!(v, Value::Null) {
        return Ok(if empty == EmptyAs::EmptyString && is_text(logical) {
            Value::String(String::new())
        } else {
            Value::Null
        });
    }
    let raw = text(v);
    let s = raw.trim();
    let bad = || {
        let shown: String = raw.chars().take(100).collect();
        Error::invalid(msg!(
            "import.bad_value",
            column = col.name,
            value = shown,
            type_name = col.data_type.native
        ))
    };
    let out = match logical {
        LogicalType::Bool => match v {
            Value::Bool(b) => Value::Bool(*b),
            _ => match s.to_lowercase().as_str() {
                "true" | "1" | YES => Value::Bool(true),
                "false" | "0" | NO => Value::Bool(false),
                _ => return Err(bad()),
            },
        },
        LogicalType::Int { bits } | LogicalType::UInt { bits } => {
            let signed = matches!(logical, LogicalType::Int { .. });
            let n: i128 = match v {
                Value::Int(n) => *n as i128,
                Value::Float(f) if f.fract() == 0.0 && f.abs() < 1e38 => *f as i128,
                _ => s.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(s).parse().map_err(|_| bad())?,
            };
            let bits = (*bits).clamp(1, 64) as u32;
            let (min, max) = if signed {
                (-(1i128 << (bits - 1)), (1i128 << (bits - 1)) - 1)
            } else {
                (0, (1i128 << bits) - 1)
            };
            if n < min || n > max {
                return Err(bad());
            }
            // 超出 i64 的无符号整数按精确小数传
            i64::try_from(n).map_or_else(|_| Value::Decimal(n.to_string()), Value::Int)
        }
        LogicalType::Float { .. } => match v {
            Value::Float(f) => Value::Float(*f),
            Value::Int(n) => Value::Float(*n as f64),
            _ => Value::Float(s.parse().ok().filter(|f: &f64| f.is_finite()).ok_or_else(bad)?),
        },
        LogicalType::Decimal { .. } => {
            if !is_decimal(s) {
                return Err(bad());
            }
            Value::Decimal(s.to_string())
        }
        LogicalType::Date => match v {
            Value::Date(d) => Value::Date(*d),
            Value::DateTime(t) => Value::Date(t.date()),
            _ => Value::Date(parse_date(s).or_else(|| parse_datetime(s).map(|t| t.date())).ok_or_else(bad)?),
        },
        LogicalType::Time { .. } => match v {
            Value::DateTime(t) => Value::Time(t.time()),
            _ => Value::Time(parse_time(s).ok_or_else(bad)?),
        },
        LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: false, .. } => match v {
            Value::Date(d) => Value::DateTime(d.and_time(NaiveTime::MIN)),
            Value::DateTime(t) => Value::DateTime(*t),
            _ => Value::DateTime(
                parse_datetime(s)
                    .or_else(|| parse_date(s).map(|d| d.and_time(NaiveTime::MIN)))
                    .ok_or_else(bad)?,
            ),
        },
        LogicalType::Timestamp { tz: true, .. } => {
            let naive = match v {
                Value::Date(d) => Some(d.and_time(NaiveTime::MIN)),
                Value::DateTime(t) => Some(*t),
                _ => None,
            };
            let t = match naive {
                Some(n) => Local.from_local_datetime(&n).earliest().map(|t| t.fixed_offset()),
                None => parse_datetime_tz(s),
            };
            Value::DateTimeTz(t.ok_or_else(bad)?)
        }
        LogicalType::Binary { .. } | LogicalType::VarBinary { .. } | LogicalType::Blob => {
            let hex = s.strip_prefix("0x").or_else(|| s.strip_prefix("0X")).ok_or_else(bad)?;
            Value::Bytes(decode_hex(hex).ok_or_else(bad)?)
        }
        LogicalType::Json => Value::Json(serde_json::from_str(&raw).map_err(|_| bad())?),
        LogicalType::Uuid => Value::Uuid(uuid::Uuid::parse_str(s).map_err(|_| bad())?),
        // 文本、时间间隔和不认识的类型按文本传（PG 上由语句转换成列的类型）
        _ => Value::String(raw.clone()),
    };
    Ok(out)
}

fn decode_hex(s: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    if !s.len().is_multiple_of(2) {
        return None;
    }
    (0..s.len()).step_by(2).map(|k| u8::from_str_radix(s.get(k..k + 2)?, 16).ok()).collect()
}
```

---

### Task 5: 服务层：后台任务（导入、执行 SQL 文件）

- `task.rs`：`TaskManager`（同时最多 4 个任务；状态、取消、等待确认、报告文件、失败的语句；结束 1 小时后清理）和上传的文件（`begin_upload` / `finish_upload`，1 小时没有使用的删除；`resolve` 解析文件来源，浏览器模式不接受本机路径）。`Service` 增加 `tasks`，数据目录下的 `tasks/`、`imports/` 启动时清空。导出仍用 M1c3 的 `ExportManager`（为避免改动已实测的导出）。
- `import/job.rs`：`Service::preview_import`、`import_ddl`、`start_import`。
  - 提交时检查：目标表（新建时由 `NewTable` 生成结构模型，主键列不可为空）、映射（同一表列不能映射两次、文件列存在）、按主键更新的条件、执行策略（拒绝时不开始）；新建表只能追加；清空后导入固定为整体一个事务。
  - 执行：另开连接；读文件在阻塞线程中按批经通道交给任务；先转换（失败的行在跳过模式下直接写入报告），再写入；
  - 整体一个事务：出错或取消回滚，并删除本次新建的表；分批提交：每批提交，出错时停止（报告出错的行号和已提交到的行）或跳过（回滚这一批后逐行重试、每行单独提交，失败的行写入出错报告，CSV 带 BOM）；
  - 写一条执行历史；新建表后清这个 schema 的元数据缓存；结束时删除上传的文件。
- `sqlfile.rs`：`Service::start_sql_file`。先扫描（拆分、按执行策略检查；被拒绝时结束，需要确认或生产环境时等用户确认），再另开连接逐条执行（可切换 schema；出错时停止或继续；可选整体一个事务，此时固定为出错时停止）；边执行边写执行日志；前 100 条失败的完整语句留在任务中；结束后清这个连接的元数据缓存。
- `session.rs`：`SessionManager::policy()`。`fake.rs`：参数中有文本 `FAIL` 时报错、`SLEEP` 时等到取消。
- 实库测试 `*_import`、`*_sql_file`（5 个库）。

后端词条加 `task.*`（5 个）、`import.*`（9 个）、`sqlfile.*`（5 个）。

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dblens-core/locales/app.json`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/fake.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/import/file.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-service/src/import/job.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/import/mod.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/lib.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/service.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/src/session.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-service/src/sqlfile.rs`
- Create: `crates/dblens-service/src/task.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-service/tests/live.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：任务 2 的 `StreamSplitter`；任务 3 的 `WriteMode`、`import_statements`、`delete_all_sql`、`create_table_sql`；任务 4 的 `FileReader`、`open_text`、`convert`、`preview_file`。
- Produces（`dblens_service`）：
  - `TaskManager`：`status(&str) -> Result<TaskStatus>`、`confirm(&str, bool)`、`cancel_or_remove(&str)`（async）、`report(&str) -> Result<(PathBuf, String)>`、`save_report(&str, &Path)`、`statement(&str, usize) -> Result<String>`、`begin_upload() -> Result<(String, PathBuf)>`、`finish_upload(&str, name, size) -> Result<UploadInfo>`、`set_dirs(reports, uploads)`；
  - `TaskStatus { id, kind, state, phase, progress: TaskProgress, warnings, error, error_line, committed_line, failures: Vec<TaskFailure>, confirm: Option<TaskConfirm>, report: Option<String> }`（Serialize）；
  - `FileSource::{Upload { upload }, Path { path }}`（untagged）；
  - `ImportRequest { profile_id, source, file, schema, table, create: Option<NewTable>, mapping: Vec<ColumnMapping>, mode, transaction, batch_size, on_error, empty_as_string }`；`NewTable { columns: Vec<NewColumn>, primary_key }`；
  - `Service::preview_import(&FileSource, &FileOptions, allow_path) -> Result<FilePreview>`、`import_ddl(profile_id, schema, table, &NewTable) -> Result<String>`、`start_import(ImportRequest, allow_path) -> Result<String>`、`start_sql_file(SqlFileRequest, allow_path) -> Result<String>`；
  - `SqlFileRequest { profile_id, source, encoding, schema, continue_on_error, transaction }`。

```diff
 async fn dameng_editing() {
```
