- [ ] **Step 10: 新建 `crates/dblens-service/src/sqlfile.rs`**

```rust
//! 执行 SQL 文件（M2b 设计文档第 6 节）：先扫描一遍（拆分、按执行策略检查），需要时等用户确认，
//! 再另开连接逐条执行；出错时停止或继续，可选整体一个事务。边执行边写执行日志。

use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Instant, SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use dblens_core::{Error, Result, msg};
use dblens_dialect::{Dialect, SqlStatement, StreamSplitter};
use serde::Deserialize;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;

use crate::history::NewHistory;
use crate::import::{TextEncoding, exec, open_text};
use crate::policy::{Decision, ExecContext, classify};
use crate::profile::{Environment, Profile, driver_name, variant_code};
use crate::service::Service;
use crate::task::{
    ConfirmItem, FileSource, SourceFile, TaskConfirm, TaskFailure, TaskHandle, TaskKind, TaskPhase,
};

/// 每次读取并拆分的文本大小。
const CHUNK: usize = 1024 * 1024;
/// 确认框中列出的语句条数。
const CONFIRM_ITEMS: usize = 20;
/// 日志中语句文本的长度上限、失败列表中语句开头的长度。
const LOG_TEXT: usize = 2000;
const HEAD_TEXT: usize = 200;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
pub struct SqlFileRequest {
    pub profile_id: String,
    pub source: FileSource,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub encoding: TextEncoding,
    /// 执行前切换到的 schema；不给时用连接的默认 schema。
    #[serde(default)]
    pub schema: Option<String>,
    /// 出错时继续执行下一条（默认停止）。
    #[serde(default)]
    pub continue_on_error: bool,
    /// 整体一个事务（此时固定为出错时停止）。
    #[serde(default)]
    pub transaction: bool,
}

fn now_ms() -> i64 {
    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map_or(0, |d| d.as_millis() as i64)
}

fn head(text: &str, n: usize) -> String {
    match text.char_indices().nth(n) {
        Some((k, _)) => format!("{}…", &text[..k]),
        None => text.to_string(),
    }
}

/// 在阻塞线程中读文件、转码，按块交出文本（附带已读字节数和文件大小）；任务丢掉接收端时停止。
fn spawn_text(
    path: std::path::PathBuf,
    encoding: TextEncoding,
) -> mpsc::Receiver<Result<(String, u64, u64)>> {
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(2);
    tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
        let file = match open_text(&path, encoding) {
            Ok(f) => f,
            Err(e) => {
                let _ = tx.blocking_send(Err(e));
                return;
            }
        };
        let (mut reader, bytes, total) = (file.reader, file.bytes, file.total);
        let mut pending: Vec<u8> = Vec::new();
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; CHUNK];
        loop {
            let n = match reader.read(&mut buf) {
                Ok(n) => n,
                Err(e) => {
                    let _ = tx.blocking_send(Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.read_failed", error = e))));
                    return;
                }
            };
            pending.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
            // 转码后是 UTF-8；块末尾被切开的字符留到下一块
            let valid = match std::str::from_utf8(&pending) {
                Ok(s) => s.len(),
                Err(e) => e.valid_up_to(),
            };
            let tail = pending.split_off(valid);
            let text = String::from_utf8(std::mem::replace(&mut pending, tail)).unwrap_or_default();
            let read = bytes.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed).min(total);
            if (!text.is_empty() || n == 0) && tx.blocking_send(Ok((text, read, total))).is_err() {
                return;
            }
            if n == 0 {
                return;
            }
        }
    });
    rx
}

/// 文件中的语句：逐块读取、拆分，按需取下一条。
struct Statements {
    rx: mpsc::Receiver<Result<(String, u64, u64)>>,
    /// 文件读完后为 None。
    splitter: Option<StreamSplitter>,
    ready: VecDeque<SqlStatement>,
}

impl Statements {
    fn open(file: &SourceFile, encoding: TextEncoding, dialect: Dialect) -> Self {
        Self {
            rx: spawn_text(file.path.clone(), encoding),
            splitter: Some(StreamSplitter::new(dialect.family())),
            ready: VecDeque::new(),
        }
    }

    /// 下一条语句；同时更新任务的已读字节。
    async fn next(&mut self, task: &TaskHandle) -> Result<Option<SqlStatement>> {
        loop {
            if let Some(st) = self.ready.pop_front() {
                return Ok(Some(st));
            }
            let Some(splitter) = self.splitter.as_mut() else { return Ok(None) };
            if task.cancelled() {
                return Err(Error::Cancelled);
            }
            if let Some(chunk) = self.rx.recv().await {
                let (text, read, total) = chunk?;
                task.update(|s| {
                    s.progress.read = read;
                    s.progress.total = total;
                });
                if !text.is_empty() {
                    self.ready.extend(splitter.feed(&text));
                    continue;
                }
            }
            // 文件读完（空块或读取线程结束）
            if let Some(sp) = self.splitter.take() {
                self.ready.extend(sp.finish());
            }
        }
    }
}

struct Run {
    profile: Profile,
    dialect: Dialect,
    req: SqlFileRequest,
    file: SourceFile,
}

impl Service {
    /// 提交执行 SQL 文件的任务，返回任务 id。
    pub async fn start_sql_file(self: &Arc<Self>, req: SqlFileRequest, allow_path: bool) -> Result<String> {
        let profile = self.profile(&req.profile_id)?;
        let password = self.secrets.get(&profile.id)?.unwrap_or_default();
        let dialect = self.metadata.dialect(&profile).await?;
        self.tasks.resolve(&req.source, allow_path, false)?;
        let task = self.tasks.create(TaskKind::SqlFile)?;
        let file = self.tasks.resolve(&req.source, allow_path, true)?;
        let mut id = String::new();
        task.update(|s| id = s.id.clone());
        let run = Run { profile, dialect, req, file };
        let service = self.clone();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let at = now_ms();
            let started = Instant::now();
            let result = service.run_sql_file(&task, &run, password, started).await;
            let (mut done, mut failed) = (0, 0);
            task.update(|s| (done, failed) = (s.progress.statements_done, s.progress.statements_failed));
            service.history.record(NewHistory {
                at,
                profile_id: run.profile.id.clone(),
                profile_name: run.profile.input.name.clone(),
                driver: driver_name(run.profile.input.driver).to_string(),
                variant: Some(variant_code(run.dialect.variant())),
                schema: run.req.schema.clone(),
                sql: format!(
                    "-- {}",
                    msg!("sqlfile.history", file = run.file.name, done = done, failed = failed).current()
                ),
                elapsed_ms: Some(started.elapsed().as_millis() as u64),
                rows: None,
                error: result.as_ref().err().map(ToString::to_string),
            });
            // 文件可能建了或删了对象：清掉这个连接的元数据缓存
            service.metadata.cache().invalidate(&run.profile.id, None);
            run.file.release();
            task.finish(result);
        });
        Ok(id)
    }

    /// 扫描：统计语句数，按执行策略检查。有被拒绝的语句时报错；需要确认时等用户回答。
    async fn scan_sql_file(&self, task: &TaskHandle, run: &Run) -> Result<()> {
        task.update(|s| s.phase = TaskPhase::Scanning);
        let ctx = ExecContext {
            profile_id: run.profile.id.clone(),
            environment: run.profile.input.environment,
            dialect: run.dialect,
        };
        let policy = self.sessions.policy();
        let mut total = 0u64;
        let mut confirm: Vec<ConfirmItem> = Vec::new();
        let mut more = 0u64;
        let mut denied: Option<(u64, String, dblens_core::Msg)> = None;
        let mut statements = Statements::open(&run.file, run.req.encoding, run.dialect);
        while let Some(st) = statements.next(task).await? {
            total += 1;
            match policy.before_execute(&ctx, &classify(&run.dialect, &st.text)).await {
                Decision::Allow => {}
                Decision::Confirm { reason } if confirm.len() < CONFIRM_ITEMS => {
                    confirm.push(ConfirmItem {
                        line: st.line as u64,
                        text: head(&st.text, HEAD_TEXT),
                        reason,
                    });
                }
                Decision::Confirm { .. } => more += 1,
                Decision::Deny { reason } => {
                    denied = Some((st.line as u64, head(&st.text, HEAD_TEXT), reason));
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        task.update(|s| s.progress.statements_total = total);
        if let Some((line, text, reason)) = denied {
            task.update(|s| s.error_line = Some(line));
            return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("sqlfile.denied", line = line, text = text, reason = reason)));
        }
        if (!confirm.is_empty() || run.profile.input.environment == Environment::Prod)
            && !task.ask(TaskConfirm { items: confirm, more }).await
        {
            return Err(Error::Cancelled);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn run_sql_file(
        &self,
        task: &TaskHandle,
        run: &Run,
        password: String,
        started: Instant,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        self.scan_sql_file(task, run).await?;
        task.update(|s| s.phase = TaskPhase::Executing);
        let stem = run.file.name.rsplit_once('.').map_or(run.file.name.as_str(), |(s, _)| s);
        let path = task.report_path("txt", format!("{stem}-log.txt"))?;
        let io = |e: std::io::Error| Error::internal(msg!("export.write_failed", error = e));
        let mut log = std::io::BufWriter::new(std::fs::File::create(&path).map_err(io)?);
        writeln!(
            log,
            "{}",
            msg!(
                "sqlfile.log_head",
                connection = run.profile.input.name,
                schema = run.req.schema.as_deref().unwrap_or("-"),
                file = run.file.name,
                at = chrono::Local::now().format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            )
            .current()
        )
        .map_err(io)?;

        let (mut conn, _) = self.registry.connect(&run.profile, password).await?;
        task.set_cancel(Some(conn.cancel_handle()))?;
        let whole = run.req.transaction;
        let stop = whole || !run.req.continue_on_error;
        let outcome: Result<()> = async {
            if let Some(schema) = &run.req.schema {
                exec(&mut conn, &run.dialect.set_schema_sql(schema), &[]).await?;
            }
            if whole {
                conn.set_autocommit(false).await?;
            }
            let mut index = 0u64;
            let mut first_error: Option<Error> = None;
            let mut statements = Statements::open(&run.file, run.req.encoding, run.dialect);
            while let Some(st) = statements.next(task).await? {
                let n = index;
                index += 1;
                match exec(&mut conn, &st.text, &[]).await {
                    Ok(affected) => task.update(|s| {
                        s.progress.statements_done += 1;
                        s.progress.affected += affected;
                    }),
                    Err(e) => {
                        if task.cancelled() {
                            return Err(Error::Cancelled);
                        }
                        let line = st.line as u64;
                        writeln!(
                            log,
                            "
{}
{}
{}",
                            msg!("sqlfile.log_failed", n = n + 1, line = line).current(),
                            e,
                            head(&st.text, LOG_TEXT)
                        )
                        .map_err(io)?;
                        task.update(|s| {
                            s.progress.statements_done += 1;
                            s.progress.statements_failed += 1;
                        });
                        let failure =
                            TaskFailure { index: n, line, error: e.clone(), text: head(&st.text, HEAD_TEXT) };
                        task.fail(failure, st.text);
                        if stop {
                            task.update(|s| s.error_line = Some(line));
                            first_error = Some(e);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            match first_error {
                Some(e) => Err(e),
                None => {
                    if whole {
                        conn.commit().await?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                }
            }
        }
        .await;
        if whole {
            if outcome.is_err() {
                let _ = conn.rollback().await;
            }
            let _ = conn.set_autocommit(true).await;
        }
        let _ = task.set_cancel(None);
        let _ = conn.close().await;
        let outcome = if task.cancelled() { Err(Error::Cancelled) } else { outcome };
        let (mut done, mut failed, mut affected) = (0, 0, 0);
        task.update(|s| {
            (done, failed, affected) =
                (s.progress.statements_done, s.progress.statements_failed, s.progress.affected)
        });
        writeln!(
            log,
            "\n{}",
            msg!(
                "sqlfile.log_summary",
                ok = done - failed,
                failed = failed,
                affected = affected,
                seconds = format!("{:.1}", started.elapsed().as_secs_f64())
            )
            .current()
        )
        .map_err(io)?;
        if let Err(e) = &outcome {
            writeln!(log, "{e}").map_err(io)?;
        }
        log.flush().map_err(io)?;
        outcome
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::time::Duration;

    use crate::fake::{FakeDriver, Log};
    use crate::history::{HistoryLog, HistoryQuery};
    use crate::policy::DefaultPolicy;
    use crate::profile::ProfileInput;
    use crate::profile::tests::input;
    use crate::registry::DriverRegistry;
    use crate::secret::{SecretBackend, Secrets};
    use crate::store::Store;
    use crate::task::{TaskState, TaskStatus};

    use super::*;

    /// 假驱动的服务，以及开发环境、生产环境各一个连接。
    fn service() -> (Arc<Service>, String, String, Log, tempfile::TempDir) {
        let store = Arc::new(Store::open_in_memory().unwrap());
        let secrets = Arc::new(Secrets::with_backend(store.clone(), SecretBackend::Vault));
        secrets.init_vault("master").unwrap();
        let log: Log = Arc::default();
        let registry = Arc::new(DriverRegistry::with_drivers(
            store.clone(),
            vec![Box::new(FakeDriver { log: log.clone() })],
        ));
        let s = Service::with_history(
            store.clone(),
            secrets,
            registry,
            Arc::new(DefaultPolicy),
            HistoryLog::inline(store),
        );
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        s.tasks.set_dirs(dir.path().join("tasks"), dir.path().join("imports"));
        let dev = s
            .create_profile(ProfileInput { environment: Environment::Dev, ..input("dev") }, Some("pw"))
            .unwrap();
        let prod = s.create_profile(input("prod"), Some("pw")).unwrap();
        (Arc::new(s), dev.id, prod.id, log, dir)
    }

    fn upload(s: &Service, sql: &str) -> FileSource {
        let (id, path) = s.tasks.begin_upload().unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, sql).unwrap();
        s.tasks.finish_upload(&id, "脚本.sql", sql.len() as u64).unwrap();
        FileSource::Upload { upload: id }
    }

    fn request(profile_id: &str, source: FileSource) -> SqlFileRequest {
        SqlFileRequest {
            profile_id: profile_id.into(),
            source,
            encoding: TextEncoding::Auto,
            schema: None,
            continue_on_error: false,
            transaction: false,
        }
    }

    async fn wait(s: &Service, id: &str) -> TaskStatus {
        for _ in 0..1000 {
            let st = s.tasks.status(id).unwrap();
            if st.state != TaskState::Running {
                return st;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
        }
        panic!("任务没有结束");
    }

    fn executed(log: &Log) -> Vec<String> {
        log.lock().unwrap().iter().filter_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("execute ").map(str::to_string)).collect()
    }

    const SCRIPT: &str =
        "CREATE TABLE a (x int);\nINSERT INTO a VALUES (1);\nFAIL;\n-- 注释\nINSERT INTO a VALUES (2);\n";

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn continue_on_error_runs_everything_and_logs_failures() {
        let (s, dev, _, log, _dir) = service();
        let req = SqlFileRequest {
            continue_on_error: true,
            schema: Some("sales".into()),
            ..request(&dev, upload(&s, SCRIPT))
        };
        let id = s.start_sql_file(req, false).await.unwrap();
        let st = wait(&s, &id).await;
        assert_eq!(st.state, TaskState::Done, "{st:?}");
        let p = &st.progress;
        assert_eq!((p.statements_total, p.statements_done, p.statements_failed, p.affected), (4, 4, 1, 3));
        assert_eq!((st.failures[0].index, st.failures[0].line), (2, 3));
        assert_eq!(s.tasks.statement(&id, 0).unwrap(), "FAIL");
        assert_eq!(executed(&log)[0], "SET search_path TO \"sales\"");
        let (file, name) = s.tasks.report(&id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(name, "脚本-log.txt");
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(file).unwrap();
        assert!(
            text.contains("Statement 3 failed (line 3)") && text.contains("3 succeeded, 1 failed"),
            "{text}"
        );
        let h = s.store.history(&HistoryQuery::default()).unwrap();
        assert!(h[0].entry.sql.contains("4 statements, 1 failed"), "{}", h[0].entry.sql);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn stop_on_error_and_whole_transaction() {
        let (s, dev, _, log, _dir) = service();
        let st = wait(&s, &s.start_sql_file(request(&dev, upload(&s, SCRIPT)), false).await.unwrap()).await;
        assert_eq!((st.state, st.error_line, st.progress.statements_done), (TaskState::Failed, Some(3), 3));
        assert!(matches!(st.error, Some(Error::Sql { .. })));
        assert!(!executed(&log).iter().any(|l| l.ends_with("VALUES (2)")), "出错后停止");
        // 整体一个事务：出错回滚；成功时提交
        log.lock().unwrap().clear();
        let req = SqlFileRequest {
            transaction: true,
            continue_on_error: true,
            ..request(&dev, upload(&s, SCRIPT))
        };
        let st = wait(&s, &s.start_sql_file(req, false).await.unwrap()).await;
        assert_eq!(st.state, TaskState::Failed, "整体事务固定为出错时停止");
        let l = log.lock().unwrap().clone();
        assert!(
            l.contains(&"autocommit false".into())
                && l.contains(&"rollback".into())
                && !l.contains(&"commit".into())
        );
        log.lock().unwrap().clear();
        let req = SqlFileRequest { transaction: true, ..request(&dev, upload(&s, "SELECT 1;\nSELECT 2")) };
        let st = wait(&s, &s.start_sql_file(req, false).await.unwrap()).await;
        assert_eq!(st.state, TaskState::Done);
        assert!(log.lock().unwrap().contains(&"commit".into()));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn production_asks_before_running() {
        let (s, _, prod, log, _dir) = service();
        let script = "INSERT INTO a VALUES (1);\nDROP TABLE a;\nDELETE FROM a;\n";
        let id = s.start_sql_file(request(&prod, upload(&s, script)), false).await.unwrap();
        let st = wait(&s, &id).await;
        assert_eq!(st.state, TaskState::Confirm);
        let c = st.confirm.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(c.items.iter().map(|i| i.line).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [2, 3]);
        assert_eq!(st.progress.statements_total, 3);
        assert!(executed(&log).is_empty(), "确认前不执行");
        s.tasks.confirm(&id, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(wait(&s, &id).await.state, TaskState::Done);
        assert_eq!(executed(&log).len(), 3);
        // 回答"否"：不执行任何语句
        log.lock().unwrap().clear();
        let id = s.start_sql_file(request(&prod, upload(&s, "SELECT 1")), false).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(wait(&s, &id).await.state, TaskState::Confirm, "生产环境总要确认");
        s.tasks.confirm(&id, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(wait(&s, &id).await.state, TaskState::Cancelled);
        assert!(executed(&log).is_empty());
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn large_file_spans_chunks_and_cancel_works() {
        let (s, dev, _, log, _dir) = service();
        let script: String = (0..60_000).map(|n| format!("INSERT INTO t VALUES ({n}, '数{n}');\n")).collect();
        assert!(script.len() > 2 * CHUNK);
        let st = wait(&s, &s.start_sql_file(request(&dev, upload(&s, &script)), false).await.unwrap()).await;
        assert_eq!((st.state, st.progress.statements_done), (TaskState::Done, 60_000));
        assert_eq!(st.progress.read, st.progress.total);
        assert_eq!(executed(&log).last().unwrap(), "INSERT INTO t VALUES (59999, '数59999')");
        let id =
            s.start_sql_file(request(&dev, upload(&s, "SELECT 1; SLEEP; SELECT 2")), false).await.unwrap();
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
        s.tasks.cancel_or_remove(&id).await.unwrap();
        let st = wait(&s, &id).await;
        assert_eq!((st.state, st.progress.statements_done), (TaskState::Cancelled, 1));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 11: 新建 `crates/dblens-service/src/task.rs`**

```rust
//! 后台任务（M2b 设计文档第 8 节）：导入和执行 SQL 文件。状态、取消、等待确认、报告文件、过期清理；
//! 以及浏览器模式上传的文件。导出任务（M1c3）仍用自己的 `ExportManager`。

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use dblens_core::{Error, Msg, Result, msg};
use dblens_driver::CancelHandle;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::sync::Notify;

/// 同时进行的任务上限。
pub const MAX_RUNNING_TASKS: usize = 4;
/// 已结束的任务、上传后没有使用的文件保留多久。
const KEEP: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3600);
/// 结果中保留的失败条数。
pub const MAX_FAILURES: usize = 100;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TaskKind {
    Import,
    SqlFile,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TaskState {
    Running,
    /// 等待用户确认（SQL 文件中有需要确认的语句）。
    Confirm,
    Done,
    Failed,
    Cancelled,
}

/// 任务进行到哪一步。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TaskPhase {
    Preparing,
    Scanning,
    Creating,
    Clearing,
    Writing,
    Executing,
    Finished,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct TaskProgress {
    /// 已读字节和文件大小（Excel 为已读行数和总行数）。
    pub read: u64,
    pub total: u64,
    pub rows_read: u64,
    pub rows_written: u64,
    pub rows_skipped: u64,
    pub statements_done: u64,
    pub statements_total: u64,
    pub statements_failed: u64,
    /// SQL 文件：影响的行数合计。
    pub affected: u64,
}

/// 一处失败：导入出错的行，或 SQL 文件中失败的语句（`index` 为语句序号，从 0 开始）。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct TaskFailure {
    pub index: u64,
    pub line: u64,
    pub error: Error,
    /// 语句开头（至多 200 字符）；导入为空。
    pub text: String,
}

/// 需要确认的语句。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct ConfirmItem {
    pub line: u64,
    pub text: String,
    pub reason: Msg,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct TaskConfirm {
    /// 至多 20 条；`more` 是其余的条数。
    pub items: Vec<ConfirmItem>,
    pub more: u64,
}

/// 任务的状态，`GET /api/tasks/{id}` 原样返回。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct TaskStatus {
    pub id: String,
    pub kind: TaskKind,
    pub state: TaskState,
    pub phase: TaskPhase,
    pub progress: TaskProgress,
    pub warnings: u64,
    /// 失败的原因；序列化时按请求的语言渲染。
    pub error: Option<Error>,
    /// 导入：出错的文件行号；分批提交时已提交的最后一行。
    pub error_line: Option<u64>,
    pub committed_line: Option<u64>,
    /// 至多 100 条。
    pub failures: Vec<TaskFailure>,
    pub confirm: Option<TaskConfirm>,
    /// 有报告文件（出错报告或执行日志）时为下载用的文件名。
    pub report: Option<String>,
}

struct Entry {
    status: Mutex<TaskStatus>,
    cancelled: AtomicBool,
    cancel: Mutex<Option<CancelHandle>>,
    answer: Mutex<Option<bool>>,
    answered: Notify,
    report: Mutex<Option<PathBuf>>,
    /// 失败语句的完整文本（与 `failures` 对应）。
    texts: Mutex<Vec<String>>,
    finished: Mutex<Option<Instant>>,
}

fn lock_error() -> Error {
    Error::internal(msg!("internal.lock_poisoned", lock = "tasks"))
}

fn not_found(id: &str) -> Error {
    Error::not_found(msg!("task.not_found", id = id))
}

/// 任务一侧的句柄：更新状态、检查取消、等待确认、写报告。
#[derive(Clone)]
pub(crate) struct TaskHandle {
    id: String,
    dir: PathBuf,
    entry: Arc<Entry>,
}

impl TaskHandle {
    pub fn update(&self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut TaskStatus)) {
        if let Ok(mut s) = self.entry.status.lock() {
            f(&mut s);
        }
    }

    pub fn cancelled(&self) -> bool {
        self.entry.cancelled.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
    }

    /// 存好连接的取消句柄（None 为清除）。先置标志再取句柄：存好之前到达的取消在这里发现。
    pub fn set_cancel(&self, h: Option<CancelHandle>) -> Result<()> {
        *self.entry.cancel.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())? = h;
        if self.cancelled() { Err(Error::Cancelled) } else { Ok(()) }
    }

    /// 进入等待确认，直到用户回答（取消算作否）。
    pub async fn ask(&self, confirm: TaskConfirm) -> bool {
        let notified = self.entry.answered.notified();
        self.update(|s| {
            s.state = TaskState::Confirm;
            s.confirm = Some(confirm);
        });
        if self.entry.answer.lock().map(|a| a.is_none()).unwrap_or(false) && !self.cancelled() {
            notified.await;
        }
        let yes = self.entry.answer.lock().ok().and_then(|a| *a).unwrap_or(false) && !self.cancelled();
        self.update(|s| {
            s.state = TaskState::Running;
            s.confirm = None;
        });
        yes
    }

    /// 报告文件的路径（任务目录下，以任务 id 命名）。`name` 是下载用的文件名。
    pub fn report_path(&self, extension: &str, name: String) -> Result<PathBuf> {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&self.dir)
            .map_err(|e| Error::internal(msg!("export.write_failed", error = e)))?;
        let path = self.dir.join(format!("{}.{extension}", self.id));
        *self.entry.report.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())? = Some(path.clone());
        self.update(|s| s.report = Some(name));
        Ok(path)
    }

    /// 记一处失败（前 100 条带完整文本）。
    pub fn fail(&self, failure: TaskFailure, full_text: String) {
        let mut keep = false;
        self.update(|s| {
            if s.failures.len() < MAX_FAILURES {
                s.failures.push(failure);
                keep = true;
            }
        });
        if keep && let Ok(mut t) = self.entry.texts.lock() {
            t.push(full_text);
        }
    }

    /// 结束：设置最终状态。失败或取消时没有报告文件的话不保留报告名。
    pub fn finish(&self, result: Result<()>) {
        self.update(|s| {
            s.phase = TaskPhase::Finished;
            s.confirm = None;
            match result {
                Ok(()) => s.state = TaskState::Done,
                Err(Error::Cancelled) => s.state = TaskState::Cancelled,
                Err(e) => {
                    s.state = TaskState::Failed;
                    s.error = Some(e);
                }
            }
        });
        if let Ok(mut f) = self.entry.finished.lock() {
            *f = Some(Instant::now());
        }
    }
}

/// 上传的文件。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct UploadInfo {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub size: u64,
}

struct Upload {
    info: UploadInfo,
    path: PathBuf,
    at: Instant,
}

/// 导入、执行 SQL 文件的文件来源：上传的文件（`{"upload": id}`），或本机路径（`{"path": …}`，只有桌面程序接受）。
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(untagged)]
pub enum FileSource {
    Upload { upload: String },
    Path { path: PathBuf },
}

/// 解析好的文件：路径、原文件名，以及任务结束后要删除的上传文件。
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub(crate) struct SourceFile {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub name: String,
    pub upload: bool,
}

impl SourceFile {
    /// 任务结束：删除上传的文件（本机文件不动）。
    pub fn release(&self) {
        if self.upload {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&self.path);
        }
    }
}

/// 全部后台任务和上传的文件。
pub struct TaskManager {
    /// 报告文件的目录、上传文件的目录。
    dirs: Mutex<(PathBuf, PathBuf)>,
    tasks: Mutex<HashMap<String, Arc<Entry>>>,
    uploads: Mutex<HashMap<String, Upload>>,
}

impl Default for TaskManager {
    fn default() -> Self {
        let tmp = std::env::temp_dir();
        Self::new(tmp.join("dblens-tasks"), tmp.join("dblens-imports"))
    }
}

impl TaskManager {
    pub fn new(reports: PathBuf, uploads: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self {
            dirs: Mutex::new((reports, uploads)),
            tasks: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
            uploads: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        }
    }

    /// 设定目录；上次运行留下的报告和上传文件一并删除。
    pub fn set_dirs(&self, reports: PathBuf, uploads: PathBuf) {
        for d in [&reports, &uploads] {
            if d.exists() {
                let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(d);
            }
        }
        if let Ok(mut d) = self.dirs.lock() {
            *d = (reports, uploads);
        }
    }

    fn dirs(&self) -> Result<(PathBuf, PathBuf)> {
        Ok(self.dirs.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.clone())
    }

    /// 删除结束超过一小时的任务和报告、上传后超过一小时没有使用的文件。
    fn purge(&self) {
        if let Ok(mut tasks) = self.tasks.lock() {
            tasks.retain(|_, e| {
                let old = e.finished.lock().ok().and_then(|f| *f).is_some_and(|t| t.elapsed() >= KEEP);
                if old && let Some(p) = e.report.lock().ok().and_then(|r| r.clone()) {
                    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(p);
                }
                !old
            });
        }
        if let Ok(mut ups) = self.uploads.lock() {
            ups.retain(|_, u| {
                let old = u.at.elapsed() >= KEEP;
                if old {
                    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&u.path);
                }
                !old
            });
        }
    }

    /// 新上传的文件：分配 id 和保存路径，由调用方写入，写完后调用 `finish_upload`。
    pub fn begin_upload(&self) -> Result<(String, PathBuf)> {
        self.purge();
        let (_, dir) = self.dirs()?;
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir)
            .map_err(|e| Error::internal(msg!("import.upload_failed", error = e)))?;
        let id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
        let path = dir.join(&id);
        Ok((id, path))
    }

    pub fn finish_upload(&self, id: &str, name: &str, size: u64) -> Result<UploadInfo> {
        let (_, dir) = self.dirs()?;
        let info = UploadInfo { id: id.to_string(), name: name.to_string(), size };
        self.uploads
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| lock_error())?
            .insert(id.to_string(), Upload { info: info.clone(), path: dir.join(id), at: Instant::now() });
        Ok(info)
    }

    /// 解析文件来源。`take` 为 true 时上传的文件交给任务（任务结束时删除），否则留着（预览用）。
    /// 浏览器模式（`allow_path` 为 false）不接受本机路径。
    pub(crate) fn resolve(&self, src: &FileSource, allow_path: bool, take: bool) -> Result<SourceFile> {
        match src {
            FileSource::Upload { upload } => {
                let mut ups = self.uploads.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?;
                let u =
                    ups.get(upload).ok_or_else(|| Error::not_found(msg!("import.no_upload", id = upload)))?;
                let file = SourceFile { path: u.path.clone(), name: u.info.name.clone(), upload: true };
                if take {
                    ups.remove(upload);
                }
                Ok(file)
            }
            FileSource::Path { path } => {
                if !allow_path {
                    return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("export.path_not_allowed")));
                }
                if !path.is_absolute() || !path.is_file() {
                    return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.bad_path", path = path.display())));
                }
                let name = path.file_name().map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
                Ok(SourceFile { path: path.clone(), name, upload: false })
            }
        }
    }

    /// 新建任务（检查同时进行的任务数）。
    pub(crate) fn create(&self, kind: TaskKind) -> Result<TaskHandle> {
        self.purge();
        let mut tasks = self.tasks.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?;
        let running = tasks
            .values()
            .filter(|e| {
                e.status.lock().is_ok_and(|s| matches!(s.state, TaskState::Running | TaskState::Confirm))
            })
            .count();
        if running >= MAX_RUNNING_TASKS {
            return Err(Error::Busy { message: msg!("task.too_many", max = MAX_RUNNING_TASKS) });
        }
        let id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
        let entry = Arc::new(Entry {
            status: Mutex::new(TaskStatus {
                id: id.clone(),
                kind,
                state: TaskState::Running,
                phase: TaskPhase::Preparing,
                progress: TaskProgress::default(),
                warnings: 0,
                error: None,
                error_line: None,
                committed_line: None,
                failures: Vec::new(),
                confirm: None,
                report: None,
            }),
            cancelled: AtomicBool::new(false),
            cancel: Mutex::new(None),
            answer: Mutex::new(None),
            answered: Notify::new(),
            report: Mutex::new(None),
            texts: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
            finished: Mutex::new(None),
        });
        tasks.insert(id.clone(), entry.clone());
        Ok(TaskHandle { id, dir: self.dirs()?.0, entry })
    }

    fn get(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Arc<Entry>> {
        self.tasks.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.get(id).cloned().ok_or_else(|| not_found(id))
    }

    pub fn status(&self, id: &str) -> Result<TaskStatus> {
        self.purge();
        Ok(self.get(id)?.status.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.clone())
    }

    /// 回答确认。
    pub fn confirm(&self, id: &str, yes: bool) -> Result<()> {
        let e = self.get(id)?;
        if e.status.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.state != TaskState::Confirm {
            return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("task.not_waiting")));
        }
        *e.answer.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())? = Some(yes);
        // 立即回到进行中：前端轮询时不会再看到确认框
        if let Ok(mut s) = e.status.lock() {
            s.state = TaskState::Running;
            s.confirm = None;
        }
        e.answered.notify_one();
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 进行中的任务：取消。已结束的任务：删除记录和报告文件。
    pub async fn cancel_or_remove(&self, id: &str) -> Result<()> {
        let e = self.get(id)?;
        let state = e.status.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.state;
        if matches!(state, TaskState::Running | TaskState::Confirm) {
            e.cancelled.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
            e.answered.notify_one();
            let handle = e.cancel.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.clone();
            if let Some(h) = handle {
                let _ = h.cancel().await;
            }
            return Ok(());
        }
        self.tasks.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.remove(id);
        if let Some(p) = e.report.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.take() {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(p);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 已结束任务的报告文件：路径和下载用的文件名。
    pub fn report(&self, id: &str) -> Result<(PathBuf, String)> {
        let e = self.get(id)?;
        let s = e.status.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.clone();
        let path = e.report.lock().map_err(|_| lock_error())?.clone();
        match (s.state, path, s.report) {
            (TaskState::Done | TaskState::Failed | TaskState::Cancelled, Some(p), Some(name))
                if p.exists() =>
            {
                Ok((p, name))
            }
            _ => Err(Error::invalid(msg!("task.no_report"))),
        }
    }

    /// 桌面版：把报告复制到用户选的路径。
    pub fn save_report(&self, id: &str, to: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        let (from, _) = self.report(id)?;
        std::fs::copy(from, to).map_err(|e| Error::internal(msg!("export.write_failed", error = e)))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// 前 100 处失败中第 `n` 条的完整语句。
    pub fn statement(&self, id: &str, n: usize) -> Result<String> {
        self.get(id)?
            .texts
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| lock_error())?
            .get(n)
            .cloned()
            .ok_or_else(|| Error::not_found(msg!("task.no_statement", n = n)))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn manager(dir: &tempfile::TempDir) -> TaskManager {
        let m = TaskManager::default();
        m.set_dirs(dir.path().join("tasks"), dir.path().join("imports"));
        m
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn confirm_cancel_and_report() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let m = manager(&dir);
        let h = m.create(TaskKind::SqlFile).unwrap();
        let id = h.id.clone();
        assert!(m.confirm(&id, true).is_err(), "没有在等待确认");
        // 等待确认：回答"是"
        let asking = tokio::spawn({
            let h = h.clone();
            async move { h.ask(TaskConfirm { items: vec![], more: 0 }).await }
        });
        while m.status(&id).unwrap().state != TaskState::Confirm {
            tokio::task::yield_now().await;
        }
        m.confirm(&id, true).unwrap();
        assert!(asking.await.unwrap());
        assert_eq!(m.status(&id).unwrap().state, TaskState::Running);
        // 报告和失败
        let path = h.report_path("log", "run.log".into()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, "log").unwrap();
        let e = || Error::invalid(msg!("task.not_waiting"));
        for n in 0..(MAX_FAILURES + 5) {
            h.fail(
                TaskFailure { index: n as u64, line: 1, error: e(), text: String::new() },
                format!("S{n}"),
            );
        }
        assert!(m.report(&id).is_err(), "进行中不能下载");
        h.finish(Ok(()));
        let st = m.status(&id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((st.state, st.failures.len()), (TaskState::Done, MAX_FAILURES));
        assert_eq!(m.statement(&id, 99).unwrap(), "S99");
        assert!(m.statement(&id, 100).is_err());
        assert_eq!(m.report(&id).unwrap().1, "run.log");
        let copy = dir.path().join("copy.log");
        m.save_report(&id, &copy).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(copy).unwrap(), "log");
        // 删除已结束的任务连同报告
        m.cancel_or_remove(&id).await.unwrap();
        assert!(!path.exists());
        assert!(matches!(m.status(&id), Err(Error::NotFound { .. })));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn cancel_while_waiting_answers_no() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let m = manager(&dir);
        let h = m.create(TaskKind::SqlFile).unwrap();
        let id = h.id.clone();
        let asking = tokio::spawn({
            let h = h.clone();
            async move { h.ask(TaskConfirm { items: vec![], more: 0 }).await }
        });
        while m.status(&id).unwrap().state != TaskState::Confirm {
            tokio::task::yield_now().await;
        }
        m.cancel_or_remove(&id).await.unwrap();
        assert!(!asking.await.unwrap());
        assert!(h.cancelled());
        assert!(matches!(h.set_cancel(None), Err(Error::Cancelled)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn uploads_and_paths() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let m = manager(&dir);
        let (id, path) = m.begin_upload().unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, "a,b\n").unwrap();
        m.finish_upload(&id, "数据.csv", 4).unwrap();
        let src = FileSource::Upload { upload: id.clone() };
        // 预览不取走，任务取走
        assert_eq!(m.resolve(&src, false, false).unwrap().name, "数据.csv");
        let f = m.resolve(&src, false, true).unwrap();
        assert!(m.resolve(&src, false, false).is_err(), "已交给任务");
        f.release();
        assert!(!path.exists());
        // 本机路径：只有桌面程序接受，必须是存在的文件
        let local = FileSource::Path { path: path.clone() };
        assert!(matches!(m.resolve(&local, false, true), Err(Error::Invalid { .. })));
        assert!(matches!(m.resolve(&local, true, true), Err(Error::Invalid { .. })), "文件不存在");
        let real = dir.path().join("x.sql");
        std::fs::write(&real, "SELECT 1").unwrap();
        let f = m.resolve(&FileSource::Path { path: real.clone() }, true, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((f.name.as_str(), f.upload), ("x.sql", false));
        f.release();
        assert!(real.exists(), "本机文件不删除");
        // JSON 形式
        let v: FileSource = serde_json::from_str(r#"{"upload":"abc"}"#).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v, FileSource::Upload { upload: "abc".into() });
    }

    #[test]
    fn running_limit() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let m = manager(&dir);
        let hs: Vec<TaskHandle> =
            (0..MAX_RUNNING_TASKS).map(|_| m.create(TaskKind::Import).unwrap()).collect();
        assert!(matches!(m.create(TaskKind::Import), Err(Error::Busy { .. })));
        hs[0].finish(Err(Error::Cancelled));
        assert_eq!(m.status(&hs[0].id).unwrap().state, TaskState::Cancelled);
        assert!(m.create(TaskKind::Import).is_ok());
    }
}
```

### Task 6: 接口与桌面版打开对话框

- `dblens-api/src/tasks.rs`：`POST /uploads?name=`（请求体直接写入文件，不受默认请求体上限限制；上限为设置项 `ui.import.upload_limit_mb`，默认 2048 MB，超过时 400 并删除已收到的部分）、`POST /imports/preview`、`POST /profiles/{id}/imports/ddl`、`POST /imports`、`POST /sql-files`、`GET`/`DELETE /tasks/{id}`、`POST /tasks/{id}/confirm`、`GET /tasks/{id}/report`（下载，任务过期前可多次下载）、`POST /tasks/{id}/report`（桌面版另存为）、`GET /tasks/{id}/statements/{n}`。
- `exports.rs`：抽出 `stream_file`（导出下载和报告下载共用）。
- `dblens-desktop`：`open_dialog` 命令（系统"打开"对话框）。

后端词条加 `import.too_large`。

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/dblens-api/src/exports.rs`
- Modify: `crates/dblens-api/src/lib.rs`

- Modify: `crates/dblens-desktop/src/shell.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：任务 5 的 `Service` 方法和 `TaskManager`。
- Produces：上述接口；`dblens_api::{UPLOAD_LIMIT_SETTING, DEFAULT_UPLOAD_LIMIT_MB}`；Tauri 命令 `open_dialog(filters) -> Option<String>`。

```diff
+/// 上传原始字节，返回状态和响应。
+async fn upload_bytes(app: &Router, name: &str, bytes: Vec<u8>) -> (StatusCode, Value) {
+    let req = Request::post(format!("/api/uploads?name={name}"))
+        .header(header::AUTHORIZATION, format!("Bearer {TOKEN}"))
+        .body(Body::from(bytes))
+        .unwrap();
+    let resp = app.clone().oneshot(req).await.unwrap();
+    let status = resp.status();
+    let bytes = resp.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes();
+    (status, serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap())
+}
+
+async fn wait_task(app: &Router, id: &str) -> Value {
+    for _ in 0..500 {
+        let (_, s) = call(app, "GET", &format!("/api/tasks/{id}"), None).await;
+        if s["state"] != "running" {
+            return s;
+        }
```

```diff
+    path: std::path::PathBuf,
+    name: &str,

+    resp.headers_mut().insert(header::CONTENT_DISPOSITION, disposition(name));
     Ok(resp)
 }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 新建 `crates/dblens-api/src/tasks.rs`**

```rust
//! 导入与执行 SQL 文件的接口（M2b 设计文档第 8 节）：上传文件、预览、建表语句、提交任务、任务状态、确认、取消、报告。

use std::path::PathBuf;

use axum::Json;
use axum::body::Body;
use axum::extract::{Path, Query, State};
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use axum::response::Response;
use dblens_core::{Error, msg};
use dblens_service::{
    FileOptions, FilePreview, FileSource, ImportRequest, NewTable, SqlFileRequest, TaskStatus, UploadInfo,
};
use futures::StreamExt;
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;

use crate::AppState;
use crate::error::ApiResult;
use crate::exports::stream_file;

/// 上传大小上限（MB）的设置项。
pub const UPLOAD_LIMIT_SETTING: &str = "ui.import.upload_limit_mb";
/// 默认上传上限 2 GB。
pub const DEFAULT_UPLOAD_LIMIT_MB: u64 = 2048;

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct UploadQuery {
    name: String,
}

/// 上传文件：请求体是文件内容，`?name=` 给出原文件名。边收边写到数据目录的 `imports/` 下。
pub async fn upload(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Query(q): Query<UploadQuery>,
    body: Body,
) -> ApiResult<(StatusCode, Json<UploadInfo>)> {
    let limit_mb = s
        .service
        .store
        .setting(UPLOAD_LIMIT_SETTING)?
        .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_UPLOAD_LIMIT_MB);
    let limit = limit_mb.saturating_mul(1024 * 1024);
    let (id, path) = s.service.tasks.begin_upload()?;
    let failed = |e: &dyn std::fmt::Display| Error::internal(msg!("import.upload_failed", error = e));
    let result = async {
        let mut file = tokio::fs::File::create(&path).await.map_err(|e| failed(&e))?;
        let mut size = 0u64;
        let mut stream = body.into_data_stream();
        while let Some(chunk) = stream.next().await {
            let chunk = chunk.map_err(|e| failed(&e))?;
            size += chunk.len() as u64;
            if size > limit {
                return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("import.too_large", limit = limit_mb)));
            }
            file.write_all(&chunk).await.map_err(|e| failed(&e))?;
        }
        file.flush().await.map_err(|e| failed(&e))?;
        Ok(size)
    }
    .await;
    match result {
        Ok(size) => Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(s.service.tasks.finish_upload(&id, &q.name, size)?))),
        Err(e) => {
            let _ = tokio::fs::remove_file(&path).await;
            Err(e.into())
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct PreviewBody {
    source: FileSource,
    file: FileOptions,
}

/// 预览文件：列名、前 100 行、推断的类型。
pub async fn preview(State(s): State<AppState>, Json(b): Json<PreviewBody>) -> ApiResult<Json<FilePreview>> {
    Ok(Json(s.service.preview_import(&b.source, &b.file, s.config.local_files).await?))
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct DdlBody {
    schema: String,
    table: String,
    create: NewTable,
}

/// 新建表的建表语句预览。
pub async fn ddl(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<String>,
    Json(b): Json<DdlBody>,
) -> ApiResult<Json<Value>> {
    let sql = s.service.import_ddl(&id, &b.schema, &b.table, &b.create).await?;
    Ok(Json(json!({ "sql": sql })))
}

/// 提交导入任务。只有桌面程序的本机服务接受本机路径。
pub async fn import(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Json(req): Json<ImportRequest>,
) -> ApiResult<(StatusCode, Json<Value>)> {
    let id = s.service.start_import(req, s.config.local_files).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(json!({ "id": id }))))
}

/// 提交执行 SQL 文件的任务。
pub async fn sql_file(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Json(req): Json<SqlFileRequest>,
) -> ApiResult<(StatusCode, Json<Value>)> {
    let id = s.service.start_sql_file(req, s.config.local_files).await?;
    Ok((StatusCode::CREATED, Json(json!({ "id": id }))))
}

pub async fn status(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Json<TaskStatus>> {
    Ok(Json(s.service.tasks.status(&id)?))
}

/// 进行中的任务取消；已结束的任务删除记录和报告。
pub async fn cancel_or_remove(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<StatusCode> {
    s.service.tasks.cancel_or_remove(&id).await?;
    Ok(StatusCode::NO_CONTENT)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct ConfirmBody {
    yes: bool,
}

pub async fn confirm(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<String>,
    Json(b): Json<ConfirmBody>,
) -> ApiResult<StatusCode> {
    s.service.tasks.confirm(&id, b.yes)?;
    Ok(StatusCode::NO_CONTENT)
}

/// 下载报告（出错报告或执行日志；链接中用 `?token=` 带令牌）。报告在任务过期前可以多次下载。
pub async fn report(State(s): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<String>) -> ApiResult<Response> {
    let (path, name) = s.service.tasks.report(&id)?;
    Ok(stream_file(path, &name, false).await?)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct SaveBody {
    path: PathBuf,
}

/// 桌面程序：把报告复制到"另存为"选的路径。
pub async fn save_report(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<String>,
    Json(b): Json<SaveBody>,
) -> ApiResult<StatusCode> {
    if !s.config.local_files {
        return Err(Error::invalid(msg!("export.path_not_allowed")).into());
    }
    s.service.tasks.save_report(&id, &b.path)?;
    Ok(StatusCode::NO_CONTENT)
}

/// 前 100 条失败中第 `n` 条的完整语句。
pub async fn statement(
    State(s): State<AppState>,
    Path((id, n)): Path<(String, usize)>,
) -> ApiResult<Json<Value>> {
    Ok(Json(json!({ "sql": s.service.tasks.statement(&id, n)? })))
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import type { ColumnInfo, TableInfo } from '../../shared/api'
import {
  autoMap,
  cellError,
  draftOf,
  draftProblem,
  fileFormat,
  fileNameOf,
  logicalOf,
  modeAllowed,
  newTable,
  sizeText,
  tableNameFromFile,
  upsertBlocked,
} from './logic'

const col = (name: string, logical: unknown): ColumnInfo => ({
  name,
  ordinal: 1,
  data_type: { native: 'x', logical },
  nullable: true,
  default: null,
  comment: null,
})

describe('导入向导', () => {
  it('按扩展名判断格式，取路径中的文件名', () => {
    expect(fileFormat('订单.CSV')).toBe('csv')
    expect(fileFormat('a.tsv')).toBe('csv')
    expect(fileFormat('b.xlsx')).toBe('excel')
    expect(fileFormat('c.xls')).toBe('excel')
    expect(fileFormat('d.json')).toBeNull()
    expect(fileNameOf('C:\\data\\订单.csv')).toBe('订单.csv')
    expect(fileNameOf('/home/u/a.xlsx')).toBe('a.xlsx')
  })

  it('自动映射：同名（不区分大小写、去空白），一个表列只用一次', () => {
    expect(autoMap([' ID ', 'Name', '备注', 'name'], ['id', 'name', 'price'])).toEqual(['id', 'name', null, null])
  })

  it('新建表的默认表名', () => {
    expect(tableNameFromFile('2026 订单-明细.csv')).toBe('t_2026_订单_明细')
    expect(tableNameFromFile('orders.xlsx')).toBe('orders')
    expect(tableNameFromFile('---.csv')).toBe('import')
  })

  it('按主键更新的条件；新建表只能追加', () => {
    const t = (pk: string[] | null): TableInfo => ({
      name: 't',
      comment: null,
      columns: [],
      primary_key: pk ? { name: null, columns: pk } : null,
    })
    expect(upsertBlocked(t(null), ['id'])).toBe('no_key')
    expect(upsertBlocked(t(['id', 'day']), ['id'])).toBe('key_unmapped')
    expect(upsertBlocked(t(['id']), ['id', 'name'])).toBeNull()
    expect(modeAllowed('upsert', false, 'no_key')).toBe(false)
    expect(modeAllowed('upsert', false, null)).toBe(true)
    expect(modeAllowed('replace', true, null)).toBe(false)
    expect(modeAllowed('append', true, null)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('预览单元格按目标列的类型校验，只查文本', () => {
    const int = col('qty', { kind: 'int', bits: 32 })
    expect(cellError({ t: 'string', v: '12' }, int)).toBeNull()
    expect(cellError({ t: 'string', v: 'abc' }, int)).not.toBeNull()
    expect(cellError({ t: 'float', v: 1.5 }, int)).toBeNull()
    expect(cellError({ t: 'null' }, int)).toBeNull()
    expect(cellError({ t: 'string', v: '2026/1/2' }, col('d', { kind: 'date' }))).toBeNull()
  })

  it('推断的类型与界面选项互转', () => {
    expect(draftOf('a', { kind: 'int', bits: 64 }).type).toBe('bigint')
    const dec = draftOf('b', { kind: 'decimal', precision: 12, scale: 3 })
    expect([dec.type, dec.precision, dec.scale]).toEqual(['decimal', 12, 3])
    expect(logicalOf(dec)).toEqual({ kind: 'decimal', precision: 12, scale: 3 })
    const s = draftOf('c', { kind: 'var_char', len: 80 })
    expect(logicalOf(s)).toEqual({ kind: 'var_char', len: 80 })
    expect(logicalOf(draftOf('d', { kind: 'timestamp', tz: false, precision: null }))).toEqual({
      kind: 'timestamp',
      tz: false,
      precision: null,
    })
    expect(draftOf('e', { kind: 'json' }).type).toBe('var_char')
  })

  it('新建表的定义：主键列不可为空；空名和重名', () => {
    const cols = [
      { ...draftOf(' id ', { kind: 'int', bits: 64 }), key: true },
      draftOf('名称', { kind: 'var_char', len: 50 }),
    ]
    expect(newTable(cols)).toEqual({
      columns: [
        { name: 'id', logical: { kind: 'int', bits: 64 }, nullable: false },
        { name: '名称', logical: { kind: 'var_char', len: 50 }, nullable: true },
      ],
      primary_key: ['id'],
    })
    expect(draftProblem(cols)).toBeNull()
    expect(draftProblem([...cols, draftOf('ID', { kind: 'text' })])).toBe('duplicate')
    expect(draftProblem([draftOf(' ', { kind: 'text' })])).toBe('empty')
  })

  it('文件大小的显示', () => {
    expect(sizeText(100)).toBe('1 KB')
    expect(sizeText(2.5 * 1024 * 1024)).toBe('2.5 MB')
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 新建 `web/src/features/import/logic.ts`**

```ts
// 导入向导的纯逻辑（M2b 设计文档第 3 节）：文件格式、列的自动映射、新建表的列和类型、预览单元格的校验。

import type { ColumnInfo, LogicalType, NewTable, TableInfo, WriteMode } from '../../shared/api'
import type { CellValue } from '../../shared/exec'
import { parseCell } from '../data/parse'

/** 按扩展名判断文件格式；不支持的返回 null。 */
export function fileFormat(name: string): 'csv' | 'excel' | null {
  const ext = name.toLowerCase().split('.').pop() ?? ''
  if (['csv', 'tsv', 'txt'].includes(ext)) return 'csv'
  if (['xlsx', 'xls'].includes(ext)) return 'excel'
  return null
}

/** 系统"打开"对话框的文件类型。 */
export const IMPORT_EXTENSIONS = ['csv', 'tsv', 'txt', 'xlsx', 'xls']

/** 路径中的文件名部分（Windows 和 Unix 的分隔符都认）。 */
export function fileNameOf(path: string): string {
  return path.split(/[\\/]/).pop() ?? path
}

const norm = (s: string) => s.trim().toLowerCase()

/** 自动映射：文件的每一列对应同名的表列（去掉首尾空白，不区分大小写），没有的为 null；一个表列只用一次。 */
export function autoMap(fileColumns: string[], tableColumns: string[]): (string | null)[] {
  const used = new Set<string>()
  return fileColumns.map((f) => {
    const hit = tableColumns.find((c) => !used.has(c) && norm(c) === norm(f))
    if (hit) used.add(hit)
    return hit ?? null
  })
}

/** 新建表的默认表名：文件名去掉扩展名；字母、数字、下划线和中文以外的字符换成下划线；以数字开头时加 `t_`。 */
export function tableNameFromFile(name: string): string {
  const stem = name.replace(/\.[^.]*$/, '')
  let s = stem.replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}_]+/gu, '_').replace(/^_+|_+$/g, '')
  if (!s) s = 'import'
  return /^\d/.test(s) ? `t_${s}` : s
}

/** 按主键更新不可选的原因：表没有主键，或主键列没有全部映射；可选时为 null。 */
export function upsertBlocked(table: TableInfo | null, mapped: string[]): 'no_key' | 'key_unmapped' | null {
  const pk = table?.primary_key?.columns ?? []
  if (!pk.length) return 'no_key'
  return pk.every((k) => mapped.includes(k)) ? null : 'key_unmapped'
}

/** 写入方式是否可用：新建表只能追加。 */
export function modeAllowed(mode: WriteMode, isNew: boolean, blocked: string | null): boolean {
  if (isNew) return mode === 'append'
  return mode !== 'upsert' || blocked === null
}

/** 预览中的一个单元格写入某列时的错误（与表数据编辑的解析规则相同）；没有错误为 null。
 * 只检查文本单元格：Excel 的数字、日期等已有类型，由后端转换。 */
export function cellError(cell: CellValue, col: ColumnInfo): string | null {
  if (cell.t !== 'string') return null
  const r = parseCell(String(cell.v), col.data_type.logical as LogicalType)
  return 'error' in r ? r.error : null
}

/** 新建表时可选的类型。 */
export const TYPE_CHOICES = ['int', 'bigint', 'decimal', 'float', 'bool', 'var_char', 'text', 'date', 'time', 'timestamp'] as const
export type TypeChoice = (typeof TYPE_CHOICES)[number]

/** 新建表的一列（界面编辑用）：长度、精度只对需要的类型有意义。 */
export interface ColumnDraft {
  name: string
  type: TypeChoice
  len: number
  precision: number
  scale: number
  nullable: boolean
  key: boolean
}

/** 推断出的逻辑类型 → 界面的选项。 */
export function draftOf(name: string, logical: LogicalType): ColumnDraft {
  const base = { name, len: 50, precision: 10, scale: 2, nullable: true, key: false }
  switch (logical.kind) {
    case 'int':
    case 'u_int':
      return { ...base, type: logical.bits > 32 ? 'bigint' : 'int' }
    case 'decimal':
      return { ...base, type: 'decimal', precision: logical.precision ?? 10, scale: logical.scale ?? 0 }
    case 'float':
      return { ...base, type: 'float' }
    case 'bool':
    case 'date':
    case 'time':
    case 'timestamp':
    case 'text':
      return { ...base, type: logical.kind }
    case 'var_char':
    case 'char':
      return { ...base, type: 'var_char', len: logical.len ?? 50 }
    default:
      return { ...base, type: 'var_char' }
  }
}

/** 界面的选项 → 逻辑类型。 */
export function logicalOf(c: ColumnDraft): LogicalType {
  switch (c.type) {
    case 'int':
      return { kind: 'int', bits: 32 }
    case 'bigint':
      return { kind: 'int', bits: 64 }
    case 'decimal':
      return { kind: 'decimal', precision: c.precision, scale: c.scale }
    case 'float':
      return { kind: 'float', bits: 64 }
    case 'var_char':
      return { kind: 'var_char', len: c.len }
    case 'time':
      return { kind: 'time', precision: null }
    case 'timestamp':
      return { kind: 'timestamp', tz: false, precision: null }
    default:
      return { kind: c.type }
  }
}

/** 新建表的定义。 */
export function newTable(columns: ColumnDraft[]): NewTable {
  return {
    columns: columns.map((c) => ({ name: c.name.trim(), logical: logicalOf(c), nullable: c.nullable && !c.key })),
    primary_key: columns.filter((c) => c.key).map((c) => c.name.trim()),
  }
}

/** 新建表的列定义有没有问题：空名、重名（不区分大小写）。 */
export function draftProblem(columns: ColumnDraft[]): 'empty' | 'duplicate' | null {
  const names = columns.map((c) => norm(c.name))
  if (names.some((n) => !n)) return 'empty'
  return new Set(names).size === names.length ? null : 'duplicate'
}

/** 字节数的显示：KB、MB。 */
export function sizeText(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`
  return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 修改 `web/src/shared/api.ts`**

```diff
--- a/web/src/shared/api.ts
+++ b/web/src/shared/api.ts
@@ -339,6 +339,104 @@ export interface FavoritePatch {
   move?: { parent_id: string | null; position: number }
 }
 
+/** 逻辑类型（dblens_core::LogicalType 的 JSON，按 kind 区分）。 */
+export type LogicalType =
+  | { kind: 'bool' | 'text' | 'blob' | 'date' | 'interval' | 'json' | 'uuid' | 'other' }
+  | { kind: 'int' | 'u_int' | 'float'; bits: number }
+  | { kind: 'decimal'; precision: number | null; scale: number | null }
+  | { kind: 'char' | 'var_char' | 'binary' | 'var_binary'; len: number | null }
+  | { kind: 'time'; precision: number | null }
+  | { kind: 'timestamp'; tz: boolean; precision: number | null }
+

+
+export interface FileOptions {
+  format: 'csv' | 'excel'
+  encoding?: TextEncoding
+  /** CSV 的分隔符；不给时自动判断。 */
+  delimiter?: string | null
+  header?: boolean
+  sheet?: string | null
+}
+
+export interface FilePreview {
+  columns: string[]
+  rows: CellValue[][]
+  /** 按前 1000 行推断的类型。 */
+  types: LogicalType[]
+  warnings: { ragged_rows: number; bad_encoding: number; error_cells: number }
+  sheets: string[]
+  encoding: string | null
+  delimiter: string | null
+}
+
+export interface NewTable {
+  columns: { name: string; logical: LogicalType; nullable: boolean }[]
+  primary_key: string[]
+}
+
+export type WriteMode = 'append' | 'replace' | 'upsert'
+
+export interface ImportRequest {

+  source: FileSource

+  table: string

+  batch_size: number

+  continue_on_error: boolean
+  transaction: boolean

+
+export interface TaskStatus {

+  phase: 'preparing' | 'scanning' | 'creating' | 'clearing' | 'writing' | 'executing' | 'finished'
+  progress: {
+    read: number
+    total: number
+    rows_read: number
+    rows_written: number
+    rows_skipped: number

+  confirm: { items: { line: number; text: string; reason: string }[]; more: number } | null
+  report: string | null
+}

+        xhr.setRequestHeader('Accept-Language', currentLang())

+          try {
+            data = xhr.responseText ? JSON.parse(xhr.responseText) : null
+          } catch {
+            data = null

+          else reject(new ApiError(xhr.status, (data as ErrorBody | null) ?? { kind: 'internal', message: `HTTP ${xhr.status}` }))
+        }

+    startSqlFile: async (req: SqlFileRequest) => (await request<{ id: string }>('POST', '/sql-files', req)).id,
+    task: (id: string) => request<TaskStatus>('GET', `/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
+    /** 进行中的任务取消；已结束的删除记录和报告。 */
+    cancelTask: (id: string) => request<null>('DELETE', `/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`),
+    confirmTask: (id: string, yes: boolean) => request<null>('POST', `/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/confirm`, { yes }),
+    /** 报告（出错报告或执行日志）的下载地址。 */

+    /** 桌面版：把报告复制到"另存为"选的路径。 */

     session: (id: number) => request<SessionInfo>('GET', `/sessions/${id}`),
```

### Task 8: 前端：导入向导、执行 SQL 文件对话框、任务通知

`features/import/`：
- `dialog.ts`：两个对话框的打开状态（`openImport({profileId, schema, table, create})`、`openSqlFile({profileId, schema})`）。
- `FileField.tsx`：桌面版用系统"打开"对话框（本机路径），浏览器模式选文件后上传并显示进度。
- `ImportDialog.tsx`：三步——选文件（CSV 的编码、分隔符、首行列名，Excel 的工作表；前 100 行预览和警告）；目标与列映射（schema；已有表时自动按列名映射、可改，预览中不能转换的值标出；新表时可改列名、类型、长度/精度、可空、主键，显示建表语句）；写入选项（写入方式，按主键更新不可选时说明原因；事务、每批行数、出错时停止或跳过；空单元格）。生产环境开始前确认。
- `SqlFileDialog.tsx`：文件、编码、schema、出错时停止或继续、整体一个事务。
- `track.tsx`：轮询任务，通知显示进度并可取消；等待确认时弹确认框（列出需要确认的语句）；结束后显示结果，可下载出错报告或执行日志（桌面版另存为）、查看失败的语句并在新查询标签中打开、打开导入的表；需要时通知对象树刷新。
- `app/ui.ts`：`refreshTree(profileId, schema)`。样式 `.form-label`、`.ddl-preview`。词条 `locales/*/import.json`（新模块）。

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/src/app/shell.css`
- Modify: `web/src/app/ui.ts`
- Create: `web/src/features/import/FileField.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/features/import/ImportDialog.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes：任务 7 的接口方法和纯逻辑；`useQueryActions().openSql`、`useTabs().openData`、`withVault`。
- Produces：`useImportDialogs`（`openImport`、`openSqlFile`）；默认导出的 `ImportDialog`、`SqlFileDialog`；`useUi().refreshTree`、`treeRefresh`。
